display_name: str, title: str = \"\", term: str = \"\") -> int:\n    if role not in ROLES:\n        raise ValueError(f\"role must be one of {ROLES}\")\n    with connect(database) as conn:\n        cur = conn.execute(\n            \"insert into users (email, display_name, title, term, role,\"\n            \" password_hash) values (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?)\",\n            (email.strip().lower(), display_name, title, term, role,\n             _hasher.hash(password)),\n        )\n        return cur.lastrowid\n\n\ndef get_user(database: Path, user_id: int) -> sqlite3.Row | None:\n    with connect(database) as conn:\n        return conn.execute(\"select * from users where id = ?\",\n                            (user_id,)).fetchone()\n\n\ndef authenticate(database: Path, email: str, password: str) -> sqlite3.Row | None:\n    \"\"\"Return the active user for these credentials, or None for any failure.\"\"\"\n    with connect(database) as conn:\n        user = conn.execute(\"select * from users where email = ?\",\n                            (email.strip().lower(),)).fetchone()\n    stored = user[\"password_hash\"] if user else _DUMMY_HASH\n    try:\n        _hasher.verify(stored, password)\n    except (VerifyMismatchError, InvalidHashError):\n        return None\n    if user is None or not user[\"is_active\"]:\n        return None\n    return user\n\"\"\"Sessions, CSRF protection and the login requirement for admin routes.\"\"\"\nfrom __future__ import annotations\n\nimport secrets\n\nfrom fastapi import HTTPException, Request\n\nfrom app import users\n\nSESSION_SECONDS = 8 * 60 * 60\n\n\nclass LoginRequired(Exception):\n    \"\"\"Raised for an anonymous (or Deactivated) visitor; becomes a redirect.\"\"\"\n\n\ndef csrf_token(request: Request) -> str:\n    token = request.session.get(\"csrf\")\n    if not token:\n        token = request.session[\"csrf\"] = secrets.token_urlsafe(32)\n    return token\n\n\nasync def verify_csrf(request: Request) -> None:\n    \"\"\"Dependency for every state-changing route: reject a missing/wrong token.\"\"\"\n    form = await request.form()\n    sent = str(form.get(\"csrf_token\", \"\"))\n    expected = request.session.get(\"csrf\", \"\")\n    if not expected or not secrets.compare_digest(sent, expected):\n        raise HTTPException(status_code=403, detail=\"Missing or invalid CSRF token\")\n\n\ndef start_session(request: Request, user_id: int) -> None:\n    request.session.clear()\n    request.session[\"user_id\"] = user_id\n    request.session[\"csrf\"] = secrets.token_urlsafe(32)\n\n\ndef current_user(request: Request):\n    \"\"\"Dependency: the logged-in, active user, or redirect to login.\"\"\"\n    user_id = request.session.get(\"user_id\")\n    user = users.get_user(request.app.state.database, user_id) if user_id else None\n    if user is None or not user[\"is_active\"]:\n        request.session.clear()\n        raise LoginRequired\n    return user\n\"\"\"Render the public site into site/ as plain HTML.\n\nT00 publishes a placeholder home page. As you build content types, extend\nrender_site() to write one file per published item. Two rules the rubric checks:\n\n  1. Only PUBLISHED content is written here. A draft that reaches site/ is a bug.\n  2. Every href and src is RELATIVE (\"style.css\", \"posts/x.html\"), never\n     root-absolute (\"/style.css\"), because Pages serves this from a subfolder.\n\"\"\"\nfrom __future__ import annotations\n\nimport shutil\nfrom pathlib import Path\n\nfrom jinja2 import Environment, FileSystemLoader, select_autoescape\n\nfrom app import settings\n\nCSS = \"\"\"/* Minimal starter styles — make them yours. */\n:root { color-scheme: light dark; }\nbody { font: 16px/1.6 system-ui, sans-serif; margin: 0 auto; max-width: 42rem; padding: 1rem; }\nheader a { font-weight: 700; text-decoration: none; }\nmain { margin-block: 2rem; }\n\"\"\"\n\n\ndef environment() -> Environment:\n    return Environment(\n        loader=FileSystemLoader(str(settings.TEMPLATES)),\n        autoescape=select_autoescape([\"html\"]),\n    )\n\n\ndef render_site(out: Path | None = None) -> Path:\n    out = out or settings.SITE\n    if out.exists():\n        shutil.rmtree(out)\n    out.mkdir(parents=True)\n    env = environment()\n    (out / \"style.css\").write_text(CSS)\n    (out / \"index.html\").write_text(\n        env.get_template(\"public/home.html\").render(\n            title=settings.SITE_TITLE, items=[], css_path=\"style.css\",\n            home_path=\"index.html\",\n        )\n    )\n    return out\n\"\"\"The shared Jinja environment.\"\"\"\nfrom fastapi.templating import Jinja2Templates\n\nfrom app import settings\n\ntemplates = Jinja2Templates(directory=str(settings.TEMPLATES))\n\"\"\"Configuration, read from the environment (never hard-code secrets).\"\"\"\nfrom __future__ import annotations\n\nimport os\nfrom pathlib import Path\n\nROOT = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1]\n\n\ndef _load_dotenv(path: Path) -> None:\n    \"\"\"Copy KEY=value lines from .env into the environment (never overriding).\"\"\"\n    if not path.exists():\n        return\n    for line in path.read_text().splitlines():\n        line = line.strip()\n        if line and not line.startswith(\"#\") and \"=\" in line:\n            key, _, value = line.partition(\"=\")\n            os.environ.setdefault(key.strip(), value.strip().strip(\"\\\"'\"))\n\n\n_load_dotenv(ROOT / \".env\")\nTEMPLATES = ROOT / \"templates\"\nSITE = ROOT / \"site\"\n\nDEV_SECRET_KEY = \"dev-only-not-for-production\"\nSECRET_KEY = os.environ.get(\"CMS_SECRET_KEY\", DEV_SECRET_KEY)\nDATABASE_PATH = Path(os.environ.get(\"CMS_DATABASE\", ROOT / \"cms.db\"))\nSITE_TITLE = os.environ.get(\"CMS_SITE_TITLE\", \"My CMS\")\n# Set this to your Pages URL once you deploy, e.g.\n# https://yourname.github.io/iphs400-mp2-cms/\nBASE_PATH = os.environ.get(\"CMS_BASE_PATH\", \"\")\n\"\"\"Login and logout.\"\"\"\nfrom __future__ import annotations\n\nfrom fastapi import APIRouter, Depends, Form, Request\nfrom fastapi.responses import RedirectResponse\n\nfrom app import users\nfrom app.security import csrf_token, start_session, verify_csrf\nfrom app.templating import templates\n\nrouter = APIRouter()\n\nREFUSAL = \"Invalid email or password.\"\n\n\ndef render_login(request: Request, error: str | None = None):\n    return templates.TemplateResponse(\n        request, \"admin/login.html\",\n        {\"title\": \"Log in\", \"csrf_token\": csrf_token(request), \"error\": error})\n\n\n@router.get(\"/login\")\ndef login_form(request: Request):\n    return render_login(request)\n\n\n@router.post(\"/login\", dependencies=[Depends(verify_csrf)])\ndef login(request: Request, email: str = Form(\"\"), password: str = Form(\"\")):\n    user = users.authenticate(request.app.state.database, email, password)\n    if user is None:\n        return render_login(request, REFUSAL)\n    start_session(request, user[\"id\"])\n    return RedirectResponse(\"/admin\", status_code=303)\n\n\n@router.post(\"/logout\", dependencies=[Depends(verify_csrf)])\ndef logout(request: Request):\n    request.session.clear()\n    return RedirectResponse(\"/login\", status_code=303)\n\"\"\"Shared test fixtures.\n\n`client` gives you the app. `client_as(role)` gives you a client that is logged\nin as a seeded user of that role — it works as soon as your login route exists,\nso access-control tests stay one line:\n\n    def test_editor_cannot_manage_users(client_as):\n        assert client_as(\"editor\").get(\"/admin/users\").status_code in (302, 403)\n\nEvery app built here runs against its own temporary database, seeded with the\ndemo users below.\n\"\"\"\nfrom __future__ import annotations\n\nimport re\n\nimport pytest\nfrom fastapi.testclient import TestClient\n\nfrom app import users\nfrom app.main import create_app\n\n# Matches scripts/seed_demo.py. Passwords come from the environment there; in\n# tests they are fixed and meaningless.\nDEMO_USERS = {\n    \"admin\": {\"email\": \"admin@example.test\", \"password\": \"test-admin-pw\",\n              \"display_name\": \"Demo Admin\"},\n    \"editor\": {\"email\": \"editor@example.test\", \"password\": \"test-editor-pw\",\n               \"display_name\": \"Demo Editor\"},\n}\n\n\ndef token_from(html: str) -> str:\n    \"\"\"Pull the CSRF token out of a rendered form.\"\"\"\n    match = re.search(r'name=\"csrf_token\" value=\"([^\"]+)\"', html)\n    assert match, \"page has no CSRF token field\"\n    return match.group(1)\n\n\n@pytest.fixture\ndef db_path(tmp_path):\n    path = tmp_path / \"test.db\"\n    users.init_db(path)\n    for role, info in DEMO_USERS.items():\n        users.create_user(path, email=info[\"email\"], password=info[\"password\"],\n                          role=role, display_name=info[\"display_name\"])\n    return path\n\n\n@pytest.fixture\ndef make_client(db_path):\n    \"\"\"Build a fresh, anonymous client against the temporary database.\"\"\"\n\n    def _make() -> TestClient:\n        return TestClient(create_app(database=db_path))\n\n    return _make\n\n\n@pytest.fixture\ndef client(make_client) -> TestClient:\n    return make_client()\n\n\n@pytest.fixture\ndef client_as(make_client):\n    \"\"\"Return a factory: client_as(\"editor\") -> a logged-in TestClient.\"\"\"\n\n    def _login(role: str) -> TestClient:\n        user = DEMO_USERS[role]\n        c = make_client()\n        form = c.get(\"/login\")\n        if form.status_code == 404:\n            pytest.skip(\"No /login route yet — build the login ticket first.\")\n        response = c.post(\"/login\", data={\"email\": user[\"email\"],\n                                          \"password\": user[\"password\"],\n                                          \"csrf_token\": token_from(form.text)},\n                          follow_redirects=False)\n        assert response.status_code in (200, 302, 303), (\n            f\"Login as {role} failed with {response.status_code}\")\n        return c\n\n    return _login\ncat: tests/auth.py: No such file or directory (os error 2)\n\"\"\"The `cms` command: serve, publish, deploy.\n\n    uv run cms serve      # admin console + public preview at http://localhost:8000\n    uv run cms publish    # render site/ from published content\n    uv run cms deploy     # push site/ to the gh-pages branch (Pages serves it)\n    uv run cms create-admin EMAIL   # make an Admin; asks for the password\n\"\"\"\nfrom __future__ import annotations\n\nimport argparse\nimport getpass\nimport sqlite3\nimport subprocess\nimport sys\n\nfrom app import settings, users\nfrom app.publish import render_site\n\n\ndef main(argv: list[str] | None = None) -> int:\n    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(prog=\"cms\")\n    sub = parser.add_subparsers(dest=\"command\", required=True)\n    serve = sub.add_parser(\"serve\", help=\"run the admin console locally\")\n    serve.add_argument(\"--port\", type=int, default=8000)\n    serve.add_argument(\"--reload\", action=\"store_true\", default=True)\n    sub.add_parser(\"publish\", help=\"render site/ from published content\")\n    deploy = sub.add_parser(\"deploy\", help=\"push site/ to gh-pages\")\n    deploy.add_argument(\"--message\", default=\"Publish site\")\n    create = sub.add_parser(\"create-admin\", help=\"create an Admin account\")\n    create.add_argument(\"email\")\n    create.add_argument(\"--name\", default=\"Admin\", help=\"display name\")\n    args = parser.parse_args(argv)\n\n    if args.command == \"create-admin\":\n        password = getpass.getpass(\"Password: \")\n        if password != getpass.getpass(\"Password again: \"):\n            print(\"The passwords did not match; nothing was created.\")\n            return 1\n        users.init_db(settings.DATABASE_PATH)\n        try:\n            users.create_user(settings.DATABASE_PATH, email=args.email,\n                              password=password, role=\"admin\",\n                              display_name=args.name)\n        except sqlite3.IntegrityError:\n            print(f\"{args.email} already has an account.\")\n            return 1\n        print(f\"Created Admin {args.email}.\")\n        return 0\n\n    if args.command == \"serve\":\n        if settings.SECRET_KEY == settings.DEV_SECRET_KEY:\n            print(\"CMS_SECRET_KEY is not set (copy .env.example to .env and \"\n                  \"change it). Refusing to serve with the public dev key.\")\n            return 1\n        import uvicorn\n\n        uvicorn.run(\"app.main:app\", port=args.port, reload=args.reload)\n        return 0\n\n    if args.command == \"publish\":\n        out = render_site()\n        print(f\"Wrote {out}. Preview it with:  python3 -m http.server -d {out} 8001\")\n        return 0\n\n    if args.command == \"deploy\":\n        if not settings.SITE.exists():\n            print(\"site/ does not exist yet — run `uv run cms publish` first.\")\n            return 1\n        result = subprocess.run(\n            [sys.executable, \"-m\", \"ghp_import\", \"-n\", \"-p\", \"-m\", args.message,\n             str(settings.SITE)],\n        )\n        if result.returncode == 0:\n            print(\"Pushed to gh-pages. Settings -> Pages -> Deploy from a branch -> \"\n                  \"gh-pages / root, then wait up to 10 minutes.\")\n        return result.returncode\n    return 1\n\n\nif __name__ == \"__main__\":\n    raise SystemExit(main())\n/usr/bin/ls: cannot access 'app/templates': No such file or directory\n/usr/bin/ls: cannot access 'app/templates/*': No such file or directory\n M notes/report-scratch.md\n M notes/usage-ledger.csv\n?? docs/transcripts/iphs400_mp2-cms_chat-session_09_faisal-syed_20261006.mdADR-001-local-admin-static-public.md  1.4K\nADR-002-stack.md  1.1K\n# ADR-001: The admin console runs locally; the public site is a static export\n\n**Status:** accepted (set by the assignment)\n**Date:** 2026-09-22\n\n## Context\n\nA CMS needs a server, a database, and login. GitHub Pages serves static files\nonly: it cannot run Python and has no database. Students have two weeks and no\nhosting budget, and a first web application with real authentication is an\nattractive target the moment it is public.\n\n## Decision\n\nThe admin console (FastAPI + SQLite) runs on the student's own machine. A\n`cms publish` step renders the published content to static HTML in `site/`, which\nis deployed to GitHub Pages on the `gh-pages` branch.\n\n## Consequences\n\n- Login, roles, and the database are never exposed to the internet.\n- The public site cannot have server-side features (search, comments) without a\n  different host; a stretch goal may add a live admin deploy behind a security gate.\n- Publishing is explicit: content changes are not live until `cms publish` runs,\n  which is a behaviour worth documenting for your client.\n- Relative paths become mandatory, because Pages serves the site from a subfolder.\n\n## Why this is an ADR\n\nIt is hard to reverse (it shapes every route, template, and deploy step) and it\nis surprising without context (most CMSes are servers). That is the bar; most\ndecisions do not meet it and belong in the spec or the glossary instead.\n# ADR-002: FastAPI + Jinja + SQLite, with no frontend framework\n\n**Status:** accepted (set by the assignment)\n**Date:** 2026-09-22\n\n## Context\n\nThe project has to be readable by someone who has never built a web application,\ngradable from a clean clone, and finishable in two weeks on a $20 plan. Options\nconsidered: Django (batteries included, but a large amount of generated code to\nread), Flask (similar to FastAPI here), a JavaScript stack (a second language and\na build step), and FastAPI with server-rendered templates.\n\n## Decision\n\nFastAPI with Jinja2 templates, SQLite for storage, argon2 for password hashing,\nand an HTML sanitizer for user Markdown. No frontend framework, no build step.\n\n## Consequences\n\n- Every page is server-rendered, so \"view source\" shows what the code produced,\n  which makes debugging visible.\n- SQLite is a single file, so a grader can seed and inspect it easily. It is\n  gitignored; `scripts/seed_demo.py` rebuilds it.\n- No build step means the file you preview is the file that ships.\n- Interactive flourishes (live preview without reload, drag-and-drop) cost more\n  here than in a JavaScript stack. That is an accepted trade.","stderr":"","interrupted":false,"isImage":false,"noOutputExpected":false},"classifierBoundary":true,"serverClassifierContext":{"request":"b4e96eb4-c172-4b86-b642-cb6599ef6607","context":{"git_state":{"cwd":"/home/faisa/code/iphs400-mp2-cms","root":"/home/faisa/code/iphs400-mp2-cms","branch":"main","default_branch":"main","status":{"clean":false,"counts":{"staged":0,"modified":2,"untracked":null,"untracked_normal":1},"porcelain":null,"truncated":false},"visibility":{"origin":{"host":"github.com","remote":"faisalsyed922-cloud/iphs400-mp2-cms","visibility":"public"},"push_remote":"origin","remotes":[{"name":"origin","host":"github.com","remote":"faisalsyed922-cloud/iphs400-mp2-cms","visibility":"public"}],"visibility_cache":[{"host":"github.com","remote":"faisalsyed922-cloud/iphs400-mp2-cms","visibility":"public"}]}},"live_cwd":"/home/faisa/code/iphs400-mp2-cms","platform":"wsl"}},"sourceToolAssistantUUID":"bfb8f3e9-b0b6-48d0-9c6b-a6d0280e113c","session_id":"4b1a37e3-ea11-48e9-baa9-b66b752b7a0d","userType":"external","entrypoint":"cli","cwd":"/home/faisa/code/iphs400-mp2-cms","sessionId":"772f668d-e085-4738-a792-8623a9d0bad6","version":"2.1.292","gitBranch":"main"}

{"parentUuid":"b460d4d5-82e4-4cd3-a185-0b59d01b8a5c","isSidechain":false,"promptId":"9e9608bf-5a8f-4433-9d69-0887f9e2a762","type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_01AsTZqHw5rjFxVvABriMGWh","type":"tool_result","content":"admin/\npublic/\ntemplates/base.html  491B\ncheck_submission.py  7.0K\nseed_demo.py  1.6K\nusage_report.py  4.3K\nbase.html  491B\nhello.html  316B\nlogin.html  474B\nhome.html  266B\n... (5 filtered)\n[+5 hidden: rtk recall 3e91a512dbd6]\n{% extends \"base.html\" %}\n{% block content %}\n  <h1>T00: hello admin</h1>\n  <p>Signed in as {{ user.display_name }} ({{ user.role }}).</p>\n  <form method=\"post\" action=\"/logout\">\n    <input type=\"hidden\" name=\"csrf_token\" value=\"{{ csrf_token }}\">\n    <button type=\"submit\">Log out</button>\n  </form>\n{% endblock %}\n{% extends \"base.html\" %}\n{% block content %}\n  <h1>Log in</h1>\n  {% if error %}<p role=\"alert\">{{ error }}</p>{% endif %}\n  <form method=\"post\" action=\"/login\">\n    <input type=\"hidden\" name=\"csrf_token\" value=\"{{ csrf_token }}\">\n    <p><label>Email <input type=\"email\" name=\"email\" required autofocus></label></p>\n    <p><label>Password <input type=\"password\" name=\"password\" required></label></p>\n    <p><button type=\"submit\">Log in</button></p>\n  </form>\n{% endblock %}\nhello.html  316B\nlogin.html  474B\n#!/usr/bin/env python3\n\"\"\"Create demo data so a grader (and you) can use the CMS immediately.\n\n    uv run python scripts/seed_demo.py\n\nT00 has nothing to seed. As you build content types, extend this so it creates:\n  - one admin and one editor (passwords read from .env, never hard-coded)\n  - a few posts and pages, at least one draft and one published\n\nThe rubric expects this to run clean on a fresh clone with .env.example values\n(item E4), because the database itself is never committed.\n\"\"\"\nfrom __future__ import annotations\n\nimport os\nimport sqlite3\nimport sys\nfrom pathlib import Path\n\nsys.path.insert(0, str(Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1]))\n\nfrom app import settings, users  # noqa: E402\n\n\ndef main() -> int:\n    admin_pw = os.environ.get(\"CMS_ADMIN_PASSWORD\")\n    editor_pw = os.environ.get(\"CMS_EDITOR_PASSWORD\")\n    if not admin_pw or not editor_pw:\n        print(\"Set CMS_ADMIN_PASSWORD and CMS_EDITOR_PASSWORD in .env \"\n              \"(copy .env.example).\")\n        return 1\n\n    users.init_db(settings.DATABASE_PATH)\n    for email, name, role, password in (\n        (\"admin@example.test\", \"Demo Admin\", \"admin\", admin_pw),\n        (\"editor@example.test\", \"Demo Editor\", \"editor\", editor_pw),\n    ):\n        try:\n            users.create_user(settings.DATABASE_PATH, email=email,\n                              password=password, role=role, display_name=name)\n            print(f\"Created {role} {email}\")\n        except sqlite3.IntegrityError:\n            print(f\"{email} already exists; left as is\")\n    # TODO (later tickets): demo Posts and Pages, including a Draft.\n    return 0\n\n\nif __name__ == \"__main__\":\n    sys.exit(main())\n\"\"\"T01: login, logout and CSRF, tested through the HTTP app.\"\"\"\nimport sqlite3\n\nfrom conftest import DEMO_USERS, token_from\n\n\ndef log_in(client, email, password, **extra):\n    token = token_from(client.get(\"/login\").text)\n    return client.post(\"/login\", data={\"email\": email, \"password\": password,\n                                       \"csrf_token\": token, **extra},\n                       follow_redirects=False)\n\n\ndef test_seeded_users_log_in_and_reach_the_console(client_as):\n    for role in (\"admin\", \"editor\"):\n        response = client_as(role).get(\"/admin\")\n        assert response.status_code == 200\n        assert \"hello admin\" in response.text.lower()\n\n\ndef test_wrong_password_and_unknown_email_get_the_same_refusal(client):\n    wrong = log_in(client, DEMO_USERS[\"admin\"][\"email\"], \"not-the-password\")\n    unknown = log_in(client, \"nobody@example.test\", \"not-the-password\")\n    assert wrong.status_code == unknown.status_code == 200\n    assert \"invalid email or password\" in wrong.text.lower()\n    assert \"invalid email or password\" in unknown.text.lower()\n\n\ndef test_failed_login_does_not_start_a_session(client):\n    log_in(client, DEMO_USERS[\"admin\"][\"email\"], \"not-the-password\")\n    assert client.get(\"/admin\", follow_redirects=False).status_code in (302, 303)\n\n\ndef test_anonymous_admin_request_redirects_to_login(client):\n    response = client.get(\"/admin\", follow_redirects=False)\n    assert response.status_code in (302, 303)\n    assert response.headers[\"location\"].endswith(\"/login\")\n    assert \"hello admin\" not in response.text.lower()\n\n\n---\nname: tdd\ndescription: Test-driven development. Use when the user wants to build features or fix bugs test-first, mentions \"red-green-refactor\", or wants integration tests.\n---\n\n# Test-Driven Development\n\nTDD is the red → green loop. This skill is the reference that makes that loop produce tests worth keeping: what a good test is, where tests go, the anti-patterns, and the rules of the loop. Every section applies on every cycle: consult them before and during the loop, not after.\n\nWhen exploring the codebase, read `GLOSSARY.md` (if it exists) so test names and interface vocabulary match the project's domain language, and respect ADRs in the area you're touching.\n\n## What a good test is\n\nTests verify behavior through public interfaces, not implementation details. Code can change entirely; tests shouldn't. A good test reads like a specification: \"user can checkout with valid cart\" tells you exactly what capability exists, and it survives refactors because it doesn't care about internal structure.\n\nSee [tests.md](tests.md) for examples and [mocking.md](mocking.md) for mocking guidelines.\n\n## Seams: where tests go\n\nA **seam** is the public boundary you test at: the interface where you observe behavior without reaching inside. Tests live at seams, never against internals.\n\n**Test only at pre-agreed seams.** Before writing any test, write down the seams under test and confirm them with the user. No test is written at an unconfirmed seam. You can't test everything, so agreeing the seams up front is how testing effort lands on the critical paths and complex logic instead of every edge case.\n\nAsk: \"What's the public interface, and which seams should we test?\"\n\nWhen the shape of that interface is itself in question (how deep the module is, where the seam belongs, what the interface should expose), call the Skill tool with \"codebase-design\" for the vocabulary. It is the shared source of the module, interface, depth, seam, adapter, leverage and locality terms, and it is a reference to consult, not a session to run.\n\n## Anti-patterns\n\n- **Implementation-coupled**: mocks internal collaborators, tests private methods, or verifies through a side channel (querying the database instead of using the interface). The tell: the test breaks when you refactor but behavior hasn't changed.\n- **Tautological**: the assertion recomputes the expected value the way the code does (`expect(add(a, b)).toBe(a + b)`, a snapshot derived by hand the same way, a constant asserted equal to itself), so it passes by construction and can never disagree with the code. Expected values must come from an independent source of truth: a known-good literal, a worked example, the spec.\n- **Horizontal slicing**: writing all tests first, then all implementation. Bulk tests verify _imagined_ behavior: you test the _shape_ of things rather than user-facing behavior, the tests go insensitive to real changes, and you commit to test structure before understanding the implementation. Work in **vertical slices** instead: one test → one implementation → repeat, each test a **tracer bullet** that responds to what the last cycle taught you.\n\n## Rules of the loop\n\n- **Red before green.** Write the failing test first, then only enough code to pass it. Don't anticipate future tests or add speculative features.\n- **One slice at a time.** One seam, one test, one minimal implementation per cycle.\n- **Refactoring is not part of the loop.** It belongs to the review stage (see the `code-review` skill), not the red → green implementation cycle.","is_error":false}]},"uuid":"7fcccbdd-aae5-48b7-b169-e391e6a2c5b7","timestamp":"2026-10-06T22:03:52.508Z","toolUseResult":{"stdout":"admin/\npublic/\ntemplates/base.html  491B\ncheck_submission.py  7.0K\nseed_demo.py  1.6K\nusage_report.py  4.3K\nbase.html  491B\nhello.html  316B\nlogin.html  474B\nhome.html  266B\n... {"parentUuid":"87a9bf1c-1578-4cb3-96e1-f6aa74dcb200","isSidechain":false,"promptId":"9e9608bf-5a8f-4433-9d69-0887f9e2a762","type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_01XenKUenZkrZ4KoJVZtybtd","type":"tool_result","content":"67. As the Admin, I want all state-changing forms to carry a CSRF token and refuse requests without it, so that another website cannot make my browser change content.\n68. As the Admin, I want Users' emails and login names kept out of the public site, so that members' personal information is never published by the CMS.\n\n### Publishing and Deploy\n\n69. As the Admin, I want `cms publish` to render only Published Posts and Pages into the static site, so that drafts never leave the database.\n70. As the Admin, I want `cms publish` to refuse and explain when any Draft would appear in the output, so that a half-written rush announcement cannot slip through.\n71. As the Admin, I want `cms publish` to show what is new, changed and removed, so that I can review a release before it goes Live.\n72. As the Admin, I want `cms publish` to render Navigation, Home, News and each Post's Byline, so that the generated site is complete and I only run one command.\n73. As the Admin, I want the News page to list Posts newest-first by published date, so that alumni see the latest chapter news first.\n74. As the Admin, I want Home to show the Home Page followed by the 5 latest Posts, so that visitors see current activity straight away.\n75. As the Admin, I want `cms deploy` to ask me to confirm before it pushes, so that I give final approval to everything that becomes Live.\n76. As the Admin, I want `cms deploy` to push only the rendered static site, so that the database, `.env` and secrets are never published.\n77. As the Admin, I want unpublished (set back to Draft) content to disappear from the site at the next release, so that taking something down is possible.\n78. As a visitor (prospective member, alumni, parent or Kenyon staff), I want to read the chapter's news, community service and history without logging in, so that I can learn about the chapter.\n\n### Demo and handover\n\n79. As a grader or a new Admin, I want the demo seed script to build a demo database with an Admin, an Editor and a mix of Draft and Published content, so that I can use the CMS from a fresh clone with no manual steps.\n80. As the outgoing president, I want a handover checklist in the README, so that my successor knows what to take over (the repository, the database and the image folder) and what to do first.\n\n### Cuttable extras (built last)\n\n81. As the Admin, I want Markdown images from an admin-managed folder to appear in Posts and Pages by relative path, so that the chapter's photos can illustrate its history and service.\n82. As the Admin, I want only images referenced by Published content to be copied into the static site, so that an unapproved photo never goes public.\n83. As an Editor, I want an optional event date on a Post, so that rush and philanthropy events show when they happen.\n84. As a visitor, I want a Post with an event date to show both its published date and \"Event: <date>\", so that I can tell when something was posted from when it happens.\n85. As the Admin, I want `cms backup` to write a dated copy of the database outside the repository, so that one dead laptop does not erase the chapter's content.\n86. As the Admin, I want a username lock for 10 minutes after 5 failed logins, so that password guessing is slow. The lock is keyed on the email.\n87. As the Admin, I want `cms deploy` to warn me and stop if content has changed since the last `cms publish`, so that stale files in the static site are not pushed by accident.\n88. As the Admin, I want the `cms publish` summary to compare against what was last Deployed, so that \"changed\" means changed since the public site was updated.\n\n## Implementation Decisions\n\n**Architecture (fixed by ADR-001 and ADR-002):** a FastAPI admin console with Jinja templates and SQLite runs locally. `cms publish` writes a static site, and `cms deploy` pushes it to the `gh-pages` branch. There is no frontend framework and no build step. This spec adds no dependency. Asking the user is required before adding one.\n\n**Modules**\n- **Users and authentication:** the User record (email, display name, title, Term, role, active flag, password hash, must-change-password flag), argon2 hashing, signed session cookies with an 8-hour lifetime, and a per-request check that rejects Deactivated Users, so old sessions stop working. Login is by email. The wrong-password message is the same whether or not the email exists.\n- **Authorization:** one place that decides \"may this User do this to this item\". Every admin route calls it on the server side. The rules:\n  - Admin may do everything.\n  - An Editor may create Posts, may edit only their own Posts, and may edit only the Pages assigned to them.\n  - Editors may set their own Posts and Assigned Pages to Published, and may set their own Posts back to Draft.\n  - Editors may delete only their own Draft Posts.\n  - Only the Admin may create or delete Pages, assign editors, set Navigation, unlock a Slug, manage Users, or reset passwords.\n  - Everything else read-only for Editors.\n- **CSRF protection:** every state-changing form carries a token, and requests without it are rejected.\n- **Content:** Posts and Pages. A Post has a title, a unique Slug, a Markdown body, status, author, created, updated and published timestamps, and a Byline snapshot (name, title, Term) taken at first Published. A Page has a title, a unique Slug, a Markdown body, status, an Assigned editor, `show_in_nav`, `nav_order`, and an `is_home` flag that is true for exactly one Page. Slugs are generated from the title, lowercase with hyphens, unique within their kind, and locked after first Published except by the Admin. Editing a Published item changes it in place.\n- **Markdown rendering:** a single function that converts Markdown to HTML and then sanitizes it, used by both Preview and the Publish run so they cannot disagree. Raw HTML is stripped. Basic formatting and links are allowed, and outside links get `rel=\"noopener\"`.\n- **Admin console:** dashboard (Pending release first, then Drafts waiting, then my recent items, and for the Admin only Pages needing an editor), content list (status, type and author filters, \"Mine\" shortcut, large status badge, read-only rows for other people's items), editor with Preview (opens in a new tab with a \"draft preview, not public\" banner, refreshed on click), user management, and the blocked-Editor message that names the Admin and the contact line.\n- **Settings:** an Admin-editable contact line shown only inside the console.\n- **Publishing:** reads only Published content, renders pages, Posts, Home, News and Navigation into the static site using relative paths only, then verifies that no Draft appears in the output. Home is the Home Page followed by the 5 latest Posts as title, date and first paragraph. News lists every Post newest-first, with titles and dates, on one page.\n- **CLI:** `cms publish` renders, shows new, changed and removed items, and refuses on any Draft in the output. `cms deploy` is separate, asks for confirmation, then pushes. `cms create-admin <email>` takes a hidden password prompt. `cms reset-password <email>` resets the Admin's own password on the machine. Both take an email, not a username, to match login.\n- **Demo data:** the seed script creates the Admin and Editor from `.env` values (never hard-coded), plus sample Posts and Pages including at least one Draft and one Published item.\n\n**Passwords and sessions:** minimum 12 characters and no other rules. Temporary passwords force a change at next login.\n\n**Cuttable extras (built last, each independent of the core)**\n- **Images:** an admin-managed folder, referenced by relative path in Markdown. The Publish run copies only images referenced by Published content. Without this extra, image syntax is stripped by the sanitizer.\n- **Event date:** an optional field on Posts. A Post with one shows both its published date and \"Event: <date>\". News is still sorted by published date.\n- **`cms backup`:** writes a timestamped copy of the database outside the repository. Never committed.\n- **Login lockout:** keyed on the email, 5 failures locks it for 10 minutes. The message does not reveal whether the email exists.\n- **Deploy fingerprints:** a successful `cms deploy` records a fingerprint of each item, the baseline for \"changed since last Deploy\" in the `cms publish` summary and for the stale-site warning in `cms deploy`. Without this extra, the summary lists every Published item.\n\n## Testing Decisions\n\n**What makes a good test:** it exercises external behavior only, such as what a User sees or is refused, and what ends up in the generated files. It never reaches into private functions or table layouts. Each acceptance criterion in a ticket should have at least one test that clearly exercises it.\n\n**Two seams, both existing:**\n1. **The HTTP app through the test client** (`client` and the logged-in `client_as(\"admin\" | \"editor\")`). This covers login and logout, roles and permission refusals (including direct URL requests), Posts and Pages (Slug rules, the Draft and Published rules, Assigned editor, Byline snapshot), the dashboard, content list and filters, Preview, user management and Deactivation, and CSRF (replaying each POST with the token removed). Assertions are on status codes, redirects and response HTML.\n2. **Static output and the `cms` commands, against a temporary site folder and a temporary database.** `render_site` covers the content of the output: only Published content, no root-absolute paths, `<script>` and `onerror=` removed, Navigation order, Home and News, bylines with no email or login name. The `cms` command entry point covers `publish` refusing when a Draft would appear and `deploy` asking before pushing, with the actual push stubbed so nothing touches `gh-pages`.\n\n**Prior art:** The shared test fixtures already provides `client` and `client_as`, and the seeded users `admin@example.test` and `editor@example.test`. The T00 tests show the style for static output tests (publish into a temp folder, then read the files and check for relative paths). The T00 tests must keep passing.\n\n**Security checklist coverage:** each of the 10 items in the project's security checklist gets at least one test through the seams above, including the editor-cannot-reach-admin-routes and anonymous-is-redirected tests.\n\n## Out of Scope\n\n- Image upload by Editors (stretch goal; for now the Admin adds photos to a folder).\n- Remote access for Editors. The console stays local (ADR-001), and live admin deployment is a stretch goal.\n- Comments, search, or any server-side feature on the public site.\n- Categories, tags and bulk actions in the content list.\n- Revision history and a separate draft copy of a Live item.\n- A public member roster page or any automated listing of members.\n- Email, including emailed password resets.\n- Pagination of News, and live-as-you-type Preview.\n- A \"protected page\" flag. Page permissions come from the Assigned editor.\n- Position-based Page assignment. Pages are assigned to a User.\n- Any change to the stack in the project's dependency list.\n\n## Further Notes\n\n- **Build order:** the 7 required capabilities come first. The five cuttable extras (images, event date, `cms backup`, login lockout, deploy fingerprints) are the last tickets so they can be cut if time runs out. The core must work without them.\n- **Vocabulary:** use the terms in `CONTEXT.md`: Officer, User, Admin, Editor, Deactivated, Term, Byline, Post, Page, Assigned editor, Draft, Published, Live, Publish run, Deploy, Pending release, Navigation and Slug. \"Published\" means ready for release and \"Live\" means actually on the public site as of the latest Deploy.\n- **Personal information:** the CMS controls only what it renders from User records. Whatever an Editor types in a body is the Editor's responsibility, and Preview shows exactly what will be public.\n- **Repo is public:** photos added to the image folder are public the moment they are pushed, so unapproved photos stay out of the folder.\n- **Source material:** the client brief and WordPress field notes in the repository's notes folder, and the grill-with-docs session decisions.\n\ndocs/mp2-grading-rubric_20260922.md\ndocs/transcripts/iphs400_mp2-cms_chat-session_05_faisal-syed_20261006.md\ndocs/transcripts/iphs400_mp2-cms_chat-session_08_faisal-syed_20261006.md\ndocs/transcripts/iphs400_mp2-cms_chat-session_07_faisal-syed_20261006.md\ndocs/transcripts/iphs400_mp2-cms_chat-session_06_faisal-syed_20261006.md\ndocs/transcripts/iphs400_mp2-cms_chat-session_09_faisal-syed_20261006.md","is_error":false}]},"uuid":"d40373f1-420d-4482-9211-5ed225e1fe57","timestamp":"2026-10-06T22:03:58.402Z","toolUseResult":{"stdout":"67. Basic formatting and links are allowed, and outside links get `rel=\"noopener\"`.\n- **Admin console:** dashboard (Pending release first, then Drafts waiting, then my recent items, and for the Admin only Pages needing an editor), content list (status, type and author filters, \"Mine\" shortcut, large status badge, read-only rows for other people's items), editor with Preview (opens in a new tab with a \"draft preview, not public\" banner, refreshed on click), user management, and the blocked-Editor message that names the Admin and the contact line.\n- **Settings:** an Admin-editable contact line shown only inside the console.\n- **Publishing:** reads only Published content, renders pages, Posts, Home, News and Navigation into the static site using relative paths only, then verifies that no Draft appears in the output. Home is the Home Page followed by the 5 latest Posts as title, date and first paragraph. News lists every Post newest-first, with titles and dates, on one page.\n- **CLI:** `cms publish` renders, shows new, changed and removed items, and refuses on any Draft in the output. `cms deploy` is separate, asks for confirmation, then pushes. `cms create-admin <email>` takes a hidden password prompt. `cms reset-password <email>` resets the Admin's own password on the machine. Both take an email, not a username, to match login.\n- **Demo data:** the seed script creates the Admin and Editor from `.env` values (never hard-coded), plus sample Posts and Pages including at least one Draft and one Published item.\n\n**Passwords and sessions:** minimum 12 characters and no other rules. Temporary passwords force a change at next login.\n\n**Cuttable extras (built last, each independent of the core)**\n- **Images:** an admin-managed folder, referenced by relative path in Markdown. The Publish run copies only images referenced by Published content. Without this extra, image syntax is stripped by the sanitizer.\n- **Event date:** an optional field on Posts. A Post with one shows both its published date and \"Event: <date>\". News is still sorted by published date.\n- **`cms backup`:** writes a timestamped copy of the database outside the repository. Never committed.\n- **Login lockout:** keyed on the email, 5 failures locks it for 10 minutes. The message does not reveal whether the email exists.\n- **Deploy fingerprints:** a successful `cms deploy` records a fingerprint of each item, the baseline for \"changed since last Deploy\" in the `cms publish` summary and for the stale-site warning in `cms deploy`. Without this extra, the summary lists every Published item.\n\n## Testing Decisions\n\n**What makes a good test:** it exercises external behavior only, such as what a User sees or is refused, and what ends up in the generated files. It never reaches into private functions or table layouts. Each acceptance criterion in a ticket should have at least one test that clearly exercises it.\n\n**Two seams, both existing:**\n1. **The HTTP app through the test client** (`client` and the logged-in `client_as(\"admin\" | \"editor\")`). This covers login and logout, roles and permission refusals (including direct URL requests), Posts and Pages (Slug rules, the Draft and Published rules, Assigned editor, Byline snapshot), the dashboard, content list and filters, Preview, user management and Deactivation, and CSRF (replaying each POST with the token removed). Assertions are on status codes, redirects and response HTML.\n2. **Static output and the `cms` commands, against a temporary site folder and a temporary database.** `render_site` covers the content of the output: only Published content, no root-absolute paths, `<script>` and `onerror=` removed, Navigation order, Home and News, bylines with no email or login name. The `cms` command entry point covers `publish` refusing when a Draft would appear and `deploy` asking before pushing, with the actual push stubbed so nothing touches `gh-pages`.\n\n**Prior art:** The shared test fixtures already provides `client` and `client_as`, and the seeded users `admin@example.test` and `editor@example.test`. The T00 tests show the style for static output tests (publish into a temp folder, then read the files and check for relative paths). The T00 tests must keep passing.\n\n**Security checklist coverage:** each of the 10 items in the project's security checklist gets at least one test through the seams above, including the editor-cannot-reach-admin-routes and anonymous-is-redirected tests.\n\n## Out of Scope\n\n- Image upload by Editors (stretch goal; for now the Admin adds photos to a folder).\n- Remote access for Editors. The console stays local (ADR-001), and live admin deployment is a stretch goal.\n- Comments, search, or any server-side feature on the public site.\n- Categories, tags and bulk actions in the content list.\n- Revision history and a separate draft copy of a Live item.\n- A public member roster page or any automated listing of members.\n- Email, including emailed password resets.\n- Pagination of News, and live-as-you-type Preview.\n- A \"protected page\" flag. Page permissions come from the Assigned editor.\n- Position-based Page assignment. Pages are assigned to a User.\n- Any change to the stack in the project's dependency list.\n\n## Further Notes\n\n- **Build order:** the 7 required capabilities come first. The five cuttable extras (images, event date, `cms backup`, login lockout, deploy fingerprints) are the last tickets so they can be cut if time runs out. The core must work without them.\n- **Vocabulary:** use the terms in `CONTEXT.md`: Officer, User, Admin, Editor, Deactivated, Term, Byline, Post, Page, Assigned editor, Draft, Published, Live, Publish run, Deploy, Pending release, Navigation and Slug. \"Published\" means ready for release and \"Live\" means actually on the public site as of the latest Deploy.\n- **Personal information:** the CMS controls only what it renders from User records. Whatever an Editor types in a body is the Editor's responsibility, and Preview shows exactly what will be public.\n- **Repo is public:** photos added to the image folder are public the moment they are pushed, so unapproved photos stay out of the folder.\n- **Source material:** the client brief and WordPress field notes in the repository's notes folder, and the grill-with-docs session decisions.\n\ndocs/mp2-grading-rubric_20260922.md\ndocs/transcripts/iphs400_mp2-cms_chat-session_05_faisal-syed_20261006.md\ndocs/transcripts/iphs400_mp2-cms_chat-session_08_faisal-syed_20261006.md\ndocs/transcripts/iphs400_mp2-cms_chat-session_07_faisal-syed_20261006.md\ndocs/transcripts/iphs400_mp2-cms_chat-session_06_faisal-syed_20261006.md\ndocs/transcripts/iphs400_mp2-cms_chat-session_09_faisal-syed_20261006.md","stderr":"","interrupted":false,"isImage":false,"noOutputExpected":false},"classifierBoundary":true,"serverClassifierContext":{"request":"90c218e9-eac2-4589-ace9-170f0e2c3376","context":{"git_state":{"cwd":"/home/faisa/code/iphs400-mp2-cms","root":"/home/faisa/code/iphs400-mp2-cms","branch":"main","default_branch":"main","status":{"clean":false,"counts":{"staged":0,"modified":2,"untracked":null,"untracked_normal":1},"porcelain":null,"truncated":false},"visibility":{"origin":{"host":"github.com","remote":"faisalsyed922-cloud/iphs400-mp2-cms","visibility":"public"},"push_remote":"origin","remotes":[{"name":"origin","host":"github.com","remote":"faisalsyed922-cloud/iphs400-mp2-cms","visibility":"public"}],"visibility_cache":[{"host":"github.com","remote":"faisalsyed922-cloud/iphs400-mp2-cms","visibility":"public"}]}},"live_cwd":"/home/faisa/code/iphs400-mp2-cms","platform":"wsl"}},"sourceToolAssistantUUID":"87a9bf1c-1578-4cb3-96e1-f6aa74dcb200","session_id":"4b1a37e3-ea11-48e9-baa9-b66b752b7a0d","userType":"external","entrypoint":"cli","cwd":"/home/faisa/code/iphs400-mp2-cms","sessionId":"772f668d-e085-4738-a792-8623a9d0bad6","version":"2.1.292","gitBranch":"main"}

{"parentUuid":"56f0c6fe-40f2-417b-8350-8a46384540f7","isSidechain":false,"message":{"model":"claude-sonnet-5-5","id":"msg_011CfmjtFqpxuanZNCz9q5wY","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"tool_use","id":"toolu_0143MH6Q3oVZSKMRgxqTrZwh","name":"Write","input":{"file_path":"/home/faisa/code/iphs400-mp2-cms/tests/test_posts.py","content":"\"\"\"T02: Posts, tested through the HTTP app.\"\"\"\nimport re\nimport sqlite3\n\nimport pytest\nfrom fastapi.testclient import TestClient\n\nfrom app import users\nfrom app.main import create_app\nfrom conftest import token_from\n\n\ndef save_post(c, title=\"Rush night\", body=\"Hello **world**\", slug=\"\", action=\"save\",\n              post_id=None, page=\"/admin/posts/new\", **extra):\n    \"\"\"Submit the post form; return the response (redirects not followed).\"\"\"\n    path = f\"/admin/posts/{post_id}\" if post_id else \"/admin/posts\"\n    form_page = f\"/admin/posts/{post_id}\" if post_id else page\n    token = token_from(c.get(form_page).text)\n    return c.post(path, data={\"title\": title, \"slug\": slug, \"body\": body,\n                              \"action\": action, \"csrf_token\": token, **extra},\n                  follow_redirects=False)\n\n\ndef created_id(response):\n    assert response.status_code == 303, response.text\n    return int(re.search(r\"/admin/posts/(\\d+)\", response.headers[\"location\"]).group(1))\n\n\ndef post_form(c, post_id, path_suffix, **data):\n    token = token_from(c.get(f\"/admin/posts/{post_id}\").text)\n    return c.post(f\"/admin/posts/{post_id}{path_suffix}\",\n                  data={\"csrf_token\": token, **data}, follow_redirects=False)\n\n\n@pytest.fixture\ndef other_editor(db_path, make_client):\n    users.create_user(db_path, email=\"other@example.test\", password=\"other-editor-pw\",\n                      role=\"editor\", display_name=\"Other Editor\")\n    c = make_client()\n    token = token_from(c.get(\"/login\").text)\n    c.post(\"/login\", data={\"email\": \"other@example.test\",\n                           \"password\": \"other-editor-pw\", \"csrf_token\": token})\n    return c\n\n\n# --- create, slug, list ---------------------------------------------------\n\ndef test_editor_creates_a_draft_with_generated_slug(client_as):\n    c = client_as(\"editor\")\n    post_id = created_id(save_post(c, title=\"Spring Rush: Kickoff!\"))\n    page = c.get(f\"/admin/posts/{post_id}\").text\n    assert \"spring-rush-kickoff\" in page\n    assert \"Draft\" in page\n    assert \"Demo Editor\" in page  # author\n    listing = c.get(\"/admin/posts\").text\n    assert \"Spring Rush: Kickoff!\" in listing\n\n\ndef test_duplicate_slug_is_refused_with_a_clear_error(client_as):\n    c = client_as(\"editor\")\n    created_id(save_post(c, title=\"Same Title\"))\n    response = save_post(c, title=\"Same Title\")\n    assert response.status_code == 400\n    assert \"already used\" in response.text.lower()\n    assert \"Same Title\" in response.text  # the form keeps what was typed\n\n\ndef test_slug_is_editable_until_first_published(client_as):\n    c = client_as(\"editor\")\n    post_id = created_id(save_post(c, title=\"Old name\"))\n    response = save_post(c, title=\"Old name\", slug=\"better-slug\", post_id=post_id)\n    assert response.status_code == 303\n    assert \"better-slug\" in c.get(f\"/admin/posts/{post_id}\").text\n\n\ndef test_posts_persist_across_a_restart(client_as, db_path):\n    created_id(save_post(client_as(\"editor\"), title=\"Survives restart\"))\n    restarted = TestClient(create_app(database=db_path))\n    token = token_from(restarted.get(\"/login\").text)\n    restarted.post(\"/login\", data={\"email\": \"admin@example.test\",\n                                   \"password\": \"test-admin-pw\", \"csrf_token\": token})\n    assert \"Survives restart\" in restarted.get(\"/admin/posts\").text\n\n\ndef test_anonymous_is_redirected_from_posts(client):\n    response = client.get(\"/admin/posts\", follow_redirects=False)\n    assert response.status_code in (302, 303)\n\n\ndef test_post_forms_require_a_csrf_token(client_as):\n    c = client_as(\"editor\")\n    response = c.post(\"/admin/posts\", data={\"title\": \"x\", \"body\": \"y\"})\n    assert response.status_code == 403\n\n\n# --- publish, byline, slug lock --------------------------------------------\n\ndef test_publishing_snapshots_the_byline_and_locks_the_slug(client_as, db_path):\n    c = client_as(\"editor\")\n    with sqlite3.connect(db_path) as conn:\n        conn.execute(\"update users set title='Secretary', term='2026–27' \"\n                     \"where email='editor@example.test'\")\n    post_id = created_id(save_post(c, title=\"News\", action=\"publish\"))\n    page = c.get(f\"/admin/posts/{post_id}\").text\n    assert \"Published\" in page\n    assert \"Demo Editor, Secretary, 2026–27\" in page\n\n    with sqlite3.connect(db_path) as conn:\n        conn.execute(\"update users set title='Alumni chair', term='2027–28' \"\n                     \"where email='editor@example.test'\")\n    page = c.get(f\"/admin/posts/{post_id}\").text\n    assert \"Demo Editor, Secretary, 2026–27\" in page  # unchanged\n\n    response = save_post(c, title=\"News\", slug=\"sneaky\", post_id=post_id)\n    assert response.status_code == 303\n    page = c.get(f\"/admin/posts/{post_id}\").text\n    assert \"sneaky\" not in page  # editor's slug change ignored once locked\n    assert 'name=\"slug\"' in page and \"disabled\" in page.split('name=\"slug\"')[1][:80] \\\n        or \"readonly\" in page.split('name=\"slug\"')[1][:80]\n\n\ndef test_admin_can_unlock_a_slug_after_first_publish(client_as):\n    editor, admin = client_as(\"editor\"), client_as(\"admin\")\n    post_id = created_id(save_post(editor, title=\"Locked\", action=\"publish\"))\n    assert post_form(editor, post_id, \"/unlock-slug\").status_code == 403\n    assert post_form(admin, post_id, \"/unlock-slug\").status_code == 303\n    save_post(editor, title=\"Locked\", slug=\"fixed-slug\", post_id=post_id)\n    assert \"fixed-slug\" in editor.get(f\"/admin/posts/{post_id}\").text\n\n\ndef test_editing_a_published_post_keeps_it_published(client_as):\n    c = client_as(\"editor\")\n    post_id = created_id(save_post(c, title=\"Live-ish\", action=\"publish\"))\n    save_post(c, Posts, tested through the HTTP app.\"\"\"\nimport re\nimport sqlite3\n\nimport pytest\nfrom fastapi.testclient import TestClient\n\nfrom app import users\nfrom app.main import create_app\nfrom conftest import token_from\n\n\ndef save_post(c, title=\"Rush night\", body=\"Hello **world**\", slug=\"\", action=\"save\",\n              post_id=None, page=\"/admin/posts/new\", **extra):\n    \"\"\"Submit the post form; return the response (redirects not followed).\"\"\"\n    path = f\"/admin/posts/{post_id}\" if post_id else \"/admin/posts\"\n    form_page = f\"/admin/posts/{post_id}\" if post_id else page\n    token = token_from(c.get(form_page).text)\n    return c.post(path, data={\"title\": title, \"slug\": slug, \"body\": body,\n                              \"action\": action, \"csrf_token\": token, **extra},\n                  follow_redirects=False)\n\n\ndef created_id(response):\n    assert response.status_code == 303, response.text\n    return int(re.search(r\"/admin/posts/(\\d+)\", response.headers[\"location\"]).group(1))\n\n\ndef post_form(c, post_id, path_suffix, **data):\n    token = token_from(c.get(f\"/admin/posts/{post_id}\").text)\n    return c.post(f\"/admin/posts/{post_id}{path_suffix}\",\n                  data={\"csrf_token\": token, **data}, follow_redirects=False)\n\n\n@pytest.fixture\ndef other_editor(db_path, make_client):\n    users.create_user(db_path, email=\"other@example.test\", password=\"other-editor-pw\",\n                      role=\"editor\", display_name=\"Other Editor\")\n    c = make_client()\n    token = token_from(c.get(\"/login\").text)\n    c.post(\"/login\", data={\"email\": \"other@example.test\",\n                           \"password\": \"other-editor-pw\", \"csrf_token\": token})\n    return c\n\n\n# --- create, slug, list ---------------------------------------------------\n\ndef test_editor_creates_a_draft_with_generated_slug(client_as):\n    c = client_as(\"editor\")\n    post_id = created_id(save_post(c, title=\"Spring Rush: Kickoff!\"))\n    page = c.get(f\"/admin/posts/{post_id}\").text\n    assert \"spring-rush-kickoff\" in page\n    assert \"Draft\" in page\n    assert \"Demo Editor\" in page  # author\n    listing = c.get(\"/admin/posts\").text\n    assert \"Spring Rush: Kickoff!\" in listing\n\n\ndef test_duplicate_slug_is_refused_with_a_clear_error(client_as):\n    c = client_as(\"editor\")\n    created_id(save_post(c, title=\"Same Title\"))\n    response = save_post(c, title=\"Same Title\")\n    assert response.status_code == 400\n    assert \"already used\" in response.text.lower()\n    assert \"Same Title\" in response.text  # the form keeps what was typed\n\n\ndef test_slug_is_editable_until_first_published(client_as):\n    c = client_as(\"editor\")\n    post_id = created_id(save_post(c, title=\"Old name\"))\n    response = save_post(c, title=\"Old name\", slug=\"better-slug\", post_id=post_id)\n    assert response.status_code == 303\n    assert \"better-slug\" in c.get(f\"/admin/posts/{post_id}\").text\n\n\ndef test_posts_persist_across_a_restart(client_as, db_path):\n    created_id(save_post(client_as(\"editor\"), title=\"Survives restart\"))\n    restarted = TestClient(create_app(database=db_path))\n    token = token_from(restarted.get(\"/login\").text)\n    restarted.post(\"/login\", data={\"email\": \"admin@example.test\",\n                                   \"password\": \"test-admin-pw\", \"csrf_token\": token})\n    assert \"Survives restart\" in restarted.get(\"/admin/posts\").text\n\n\ndef test_anonymous_is_redirected_from_posts(client):\n    response = client.get(\"/admin/posts\", follow_redirects=False)\n    assert response.status_code in (302, 303)\n\n\ndef test_post_forms_require_a_csrf_token(client_as):\n    c = client_as(\"editor\")\n    response = c.post(\"/admin/posts\", data={\"title\": \"x\", \"body\": \"y\"})\n    assert response.status_code == 403\n\n\n# --- publish, byline, slug lock --------------------------------------------\n\ndef test_publishing_snapshots_the_byline_and_locks_the_slug(client_as, db_path):\n    c = client_as(\"editor\")\n    with sqlite3.connect(db_path) as conn:\n        conn.execute(\"update users set title='Secretary', term='2026–27' \"\n                     \"where email='editor@example.test'\")\n    post_id = created_id(save_post(c, title=\"News\", action=\"publish\"))\n    page = c.get(f\"/admin/posts/{post_id}\").text\n    assert \"Published\" in page\n    assert \"Demo Editor, Secretary, 2026–27\" in page\n\n    with sqlite3.connect(db_path) as conn:\n        conn.execute(\"update users set title='Alumni chair', term='2027–28' \"\n                     \"where email='editor@example.test'\")\n    page = c.get(f\"/admin/posts/{post_id}\").text\n    assert \"Demo Editor, Secretary, 2026–27\" in page  # unchanged\n\n    response = save_post(c, title=\"News\", slug=\"sneaky\", post_id=post_id)\n    assert response.status_code == 303\n    page = c.get(f\"/admin/posts/{post_id}\").text\n    assert \"sneaky\" not in page  # editor's slug change ignored once locked\n    assert 'name=\"slug\"' in page and \"disabled\" in page.split('name=\"slug\"')[1][:80] \\\n        or \"readonly\" in page.split('name=\"slug\"')[1][:80]\n\n\ndef test_admin_can_unlock_a_slug_after_first_publish(client_as):\n    editor, admin = client_as(\"editor\"), client_as(\"admin\")\n    post_id = created_id(save_post(editor, title=\"Locked\", action=\"publish\"))\n    assert post_form(editor, post_id, \"/unlock-slug\").status_code == 403\n    assert post_form(admin, post_id, \"/unlock-slug\").status_code == 303\n    save_post(editor, title=\"Locked\", slug=\"fixed-slug\", post_id=post_id)\n    assert \"fixed-slug\" in editor.get(f\"/admin/posts/{post_id}\").text\n\n\ndef test_editing_a_published_post_keeps_it_published(client_as):\n    c = client_as(\"editor\")\n    post_id = created_id(save_post(c, title=\"Live-ish\", action=\"publish\"))\n    save_post(c, title=\"Live-ish fixed\", post_id=post_id)\n    page = c.get(f\"/admin/posts/{post_id}\").text\n    assert \"Live-ish fixed\" in page\n    assert 'data-status=\"published\"' in page\n\n\ndef test_editor_can_set_their_own_post_back_to_draft(client_as):\n    c = client_as(\"editor\")\n    post_id = created_id(save_post(c, title=\"Oops\", action=\"publish\"))\n    save_post(c, test_post_forms_require_a_csrf_token(client_as):\n    c = client_as(\"editor\")\n    response = c.post(\"/admin/posts\", data={\"title\": \"x\", \"body\": \"y\"})\n    assert response.status_code == 403\n\n\n# --- publish, byline, slug lock --------------------------------------------\n\ndef test_publishing_snapshots_the_byline_and_locks_the_slug(client_as, db_path):\n    c = client_as(\"editor\")\n    with sqlite3.connect(db_path) as conn:\n        conn.execute(\"update users set title='Secretary', term='2026–27' \"\n                     \"where email='editor@example.test'\")\n    post_id = created_id(save_post(c, title=\"News\", action=\"publish\"))\n    page = c.get(f\"/admin/posts/{post_id}\").text\n    assert \"Published\" in page\n    assert \"Demo Editor, Secretary, 2026–27\" in page\n\n    with sqlite3.connect(db_path) as conn:\n        conn.execute(\"update users set title='Alumni chair', term='2027–28' \"\n                     \"where email='editor@example.test'\")\n    page = c.get(f\"/admin/posts/{post_id}\").text\n    assert \"Demo Editor, Secretary, 2026–27\" in page  # unchanged\n\n    response = save_post(c, title=\"News\", slug=\"sneaky\", post_id=post_id)\n    assert response.status_code == 303\n    page = c.get(f\"/admin/posts/{post_id}\").text\n    assert \"sneaky\" not in page  # editor's slug change ignored once locked\n    assert 'name=\"slug\"' in page and \"disabled\" in page.split('name=\"slug\"')[1][:80] \\\n        or \"readonly\" in page.split('name=\"slug\"')[1][:80]\n\n\ndef test_admin_can_unlock_a_slug_after_first_publish(client_as):\n    editor, admin = client_as(\"editor\"), client_as(\"admin\")\n    post_id = created_id(save_post(editor, title=\"Locked\", action=\"publish\"))\n    assert post_form(editor, post_id, \"/unlock-slug\").status_code == 403\n    assert post_form(admin, post_id, \"/unlock-slug\").status_code == 303\n    save_post(editor, title=\"Locked\", slug=\"fixed-slug\", post_id=post_id)\n    assert \"fixed-slug\" in editor.get(f\"/admin/posts/{post_id}\").text\n\n\ndef test_editing_a_published_post_keeps_it_published(client_as):\n    c = client_as(\"editor\")\n    post_id = created_id(save_post(c, title=\"Live-ish\", action=\"publish\"))\n    save_post(c, title=\"Live-ish fixed\", post_id=post_id)\n    page = c.get(f\"/admin/posts/{post_id}\").text\n    assert \"Live-ish fixed\" in page\n    assert 'data-status=\"published\"' in page\n\n\ndef test_editor_can_set_their_own_post_back_to_draft(client_as):\n    c = client_as(\"editor\")\n    post_id = created_id(save_post(c, title=\"Oops\", action=\"publish\"))\n    save_post(c, title=\"Oops\", action=\"draft\", post_id=post_id)\n    assert 'data-status=\"draft\"' in c.get(f\"/admin/posts/{post_id}\").text\n\n\n# --- permissions -------------------------------------------------------------\n\ndef test_editor_cannot_view_edit_or_delete_another_editors_post(client_as, other_editor):\n    post_id = created_id(save_post(client_as(\"editor\"), title=\"Mine\"))\n    assert other_editor.get(f\"/admin/posts/{post_id}\").status_code == 403\n    assert save_post(other_editor, title=\"Hijack\", post_id=post_id,\n                     page=\"/admin/posts/new\").status_code == 403\n    token = token_from(other_editor.get(\"/admin/posts/new\").text)\n    response = created_id(save_post(client_as(\"editor\"), title=\"Someone elses post\"))\n    assert \"Someone elses post\" in other_editor.get(\"/admin/posts\").text\n\n\ndef test_editor_deletes_own_draft_but_not_a_published_post(client_as):\n    c = client_as(\"editor\")\n    draft = created_id(save_post(c, title=\"Abandoned\"))\n    live = created_id(save_post(c, {"parentUuid":"e2fb0c99-53af-4923-86b9-b115f42e98c9","isSidechain":false,"message":{"model":"claude-sonnet-5-5","id":"msg_011Cfmjwbd3959YKgzzCU3eW","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"tool_use","id":"toolu_01NH2SCGSUVcYRAAUBieVzjh","name":"Bash","input":{"command":"cat > app/markdown.py <<'EOF'\n\"\"\"Markdown -> sanitized HTML. Preview and the Publish run both call this.\"\"\"\nfrom __future__ import annotations\n\nimport nh3\nfrom markdown_it import MarkdownIt\n\n_md = MarkdownIt(\"commonmark\", {\"html\": True})\n\n_TAGS = {\"p\", \"br\", \"h1\", \"h2\", \"h3\", \"h4\", \"h5\", \"h6\", \"strong\", \"em\", \"ul\", \"ol\",\n         \"li\", \"blockquote\", \"a\", \"code\", \"pre\", \"hr\"}\n_ATTRIBUTES = {\"a\": {\"href\", \"title\"}}\n\n\ndef render_markdown(text: str) -> str:\n    \"\"\"Render Markdown, then strip everything outside the allowlist.\n\n    Raw HTML is removed (scripts, event handlers, images). Links keep only\n    http, https and mailto URLs and always get rel=\"noopener noreferrer\".\n    \"\"\"\n    return nh3.clean(\n        _md.render(text or \"\"),\n        tags=_TAGS,\n        attributes=_ATTRIBUTES,\n        url_schemes={\"http\", \"https\", \"mailto\"},\n        link_rel=\"noopener noreferrer\",\n    )\nEOF\ncat > app/authz.py <<'EOF'\n\"\"\"One place that decides \"may this User do this to this item\".\"\"\"\nfrom __future__ import annotations\n\n\ndef is_admin(user) -> bool:\n    return user[\"role\"] == \"admin\"\n\n\ndef can_edit_post(user, post) -> bool:\n    return is_admin(user) or post[\"author_id\"] == user[\"id\"]\n\n\ndef can_delete_post(user, post) -> bool:\n    if is_admin(user):\n        return True\n    return post[\"author_id\"] == user[\"id\"] and post[\"status\"] == \"draft\"\n\n\ndef can_edit_slug(user, post) -> bool:\n    \"\"\"Admin always; an Editor only while the Slug is not locked.\"\"\"\n    return is_admin(user) or not post[\"slug_locked\"]\n\n\ndef can_unlock_slug(user) -> bool:\n    return is_admin(user)\nEOF\ncat > app/posts.py <<'EOF'\n\"\"\"Post storage. A Post's Byline is copied from its author at first Published.\"\"\"\nfrom __future__ import annotations\n\nimport re\nimport sqlite3\nimport unicodedata\nfrom datetime import datetime, timezone\nfrom pathlib import Path\n\nfrom app.users import connect\n\n_SCHEMA = \"\"\"\ncreate table if not exists posts (\n    id integer primary key,\n    title text not null,\n    slug text not null unique,\n    body text not null default '',\n    status text not null default 'draft' check (status in ('draft', 'published')),\n    author_id integer not null references users(id),\n    created_at text not null,\n    updated_at text not null,\n    published_at text,\n    slug_locked integer not null default 0,\n    byline_name text,\n    byline_title text,\n    byline_term text\n)\n\"\"\"\n\n\nclass DuplicateSlug(Exception):\n    pass\n\n\ndef now() -> str:\n    return datetime.now(timezone.utc).isoformat(timespec=\"seconds\")\n\n\ndef slugify(title: str) -> str:\n    text = unicodedata.normalize(\"NFKD\", title).encode(\"ascii\", \"ignore\").decode()\n    return re.sub(r\"[^a-z0-9]+\", \"-\", text.lower()).strip(\"-\") or \"post\"\n\n\ndef init_posts(database: Path) -> None:\n    with connect(database) as conn:\n        conn.execute(_SCHEMA)\n\n\ndef get_post(database: Path, post_id: int) -> sqlite3.Row | None:\n    with connect(database) as conn:\n        return conn.execute(\n            \"select posts.*, users.display_name as author_name from posts\"\n            \" join users on users.id = posts.author_id where posts.id = ?\",\n            (post_id,)).fetchone()\n\n\ndef list_posts(database: Path) -> list[sqlite3.Row]:\n    with connect(database) as conn:\n        return conn.execute(\n            \"select posts.*, users.display_name as author_name from posts\"\n            \" join users on users.id = posts.author_id\"\n            \" order by posts.updated_at desc, posts.id desc\").fetchall()\n\n\ndef create_post(database: Path, *, author, title: str, slug: str, body: str,\n                publish: bool = False) -> int:\n    stamp = now()\n    try:\n        with connect(database) as conn:\n            cur = conn.execute(\n                \"insert into posts (title, slug, body, author_id, created_at,\"\n                \" updated_at) values (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?)\",\n                (title, slug or slugify(title), body, author[\"id\"], stamp, stamp))\n            post_id = cur.lastrowid\n            if publish:\n                _publish(conn, post_id, author, stamp)\n            return post_id\n    except sqlite3.IntegrityError as exc:\n        if \"posts.slug\" in str(exc):\n            raise DuplicateSlug from exc\n        raise\n\n\ndef update_post(database: Path, post_id: int, *, title: str, body: str,\n                slug: str | None, action: str) -> None:\n    \"\"\"Save changes. `slug` is None when the caller may not change it.\"\"\"\n    stamp = now()\n    try:\n        with connect(database) as conn:\n            if slug is None:\n                conn.execute(\"update posts set title=?, body=?, updated_at=?\"\n                             \" where id=?\", (title, body, stamp, post_id))\n            else:\n                conn.execute(\"update posts set title=?, body=?, slug=?,\"\n                             \" updated_at=? where id=?\",\n                             (title, body, slug or slugify(title), stamp, post_id))\n            if action == \"publish\":\n                author = conn.execute(\n                    \"select users.* from users join posts\"\n                    \" on posts.author_id = users.id where posts.id=?\",\n                    (post_id,)).fetchone()\n                _publish(conn, post_id, author, stamp)\n            elif action == \"draft\":\n                conn.execute(\"update posts set status='draft' where id=?\",\n                             (post_id,))\n    except sqlite3.IntegrityError as exc:\n        if \"posts.slug\" in str(exc):\n            raise DuplicateSlug from exc\n        raise\n\n\ndef _publish(conn: sqlite3.Connection, post_id: int, author, stamp: str) -> None:\n    first = conn.execute(\"select published_at from posts where id=?\",\n                         (post_id,)).fetchone()[\"published_at\"] is None\n    conn.execute(\"update posts set status='published' where id=?\", (post_id,))\n /home/faisa/code/iphs400-mp2-cms && cat > app/markdown.py <<'EOF'\n\"\"\"Markdown -> sanitized HTML. Preview and the Publish run both call this.\"\"\"\nfrom __future__ import annotations\n\nimport nh3\nfrom markdown_it import MarkdownIt\n\n_md = MarkdownIt(\"commonmark\", {\"html\": True})\n\n_TAGS = {\"p\", \"br\", \"h1\", \"h2\", \"h3\", \"h4\", \"h5\", \"h6\", \"strong\", \"em\", \"ul\", \"ol\",\n         \"li\", \"blockquote\", \"a\", \"code\", \"pre\", \"hr\"}\n_ATTRIBUTES = {\"a\": {\"href\", \"title\"}}\n\n\ndef render_markdown(text: str) -> str:\n    \"\"\"Render Markdown, then strip everything outside the allowlist.\n\n    Raw HTML is removed (scripts, event handlers, images). Links keep only\n    http, https and mailto URLs and always get rel=\"noopener noreferrer\".\n    \"\"\"\n    return nh3.clean(\n        _md.render(text or \"\"),\n        tags=_TAGS,\n        attributes=_ATTRIBUTES,\n        url_schemes={\"http\", \"https\", \"mailto\"},\n        link_rel=\"noopener noreferrer\",\n    )\nEOF\ncat > app/authz.py <<'EOF'\n\"\"\"One place that decides \"may this User do this to this item\".\"\"\"\nfrom __future__ import annotations\n\n\ndef is_admin(user) -> bool:\n    return user[\"role\"] == \"admin\"\n\n\ndef can_edit_post(user, post) -> bool:\n    return is_admin(user) or post[\"author_id\"] == user[\"id\"]\n\n\ndef can_delete_post(user, post) -> bool:\n    if is_admin(user):\n        return True\n    return post[\"author_id\"] == user[\"id\"] and post[\"status\"] == \"draft\"\n\n\ndef can_edit_slug(user, post) -> bool:\n    \"\"\"Admin always; an Editor only while the Slug is not locked.\"\"\"\n    return is_admin(user) or not post[\"slug_locked\"]\n\n\ndef can_unlock_slug(user) -> bool:\n    return is_admin(user)\nEOF\ncat > app/posts.py <<'EOF'\n\"\"\"Post storage. A Post's Byline is copied from its author at first Published.\"\"\"\nfrom __future__ import annotations\n\nimport re\nimport sqlite3\nimport unicodedata\nfrom datetime import datetime, timezone\nfrom pathlib import Path\n\nfrom app.users import connect\n\n_SCHEMA = \"\"\"\ncreate table if not exists posts (\n    id integer primary key,\n    title text not null,\n    slug text not null unique,\n    body text not null default '',\n    status text not null default 'draft' check (status in ('draft', 'published')),\n    author_id integer not null references users(id),\n    created_at text not null,\n    updated_at text not null,\n    published_at text,\n    slug_locked integer not null default 0,\n    byline_name text,\n    byline_title text,\n    byline_term text\n)\n\"\"\"\n\n\nclass DuplicateSlug(Exception):\n    pass\n\n\ndef now() -> str:\n    return datetime.now(timezone.utc).isoformat(timespec=\"seconds\")\n\n\ndef slugify(title: str) -> str:\n    text = unicodedata.normalize(\"NFKD\", title).encode(\"ascii\", \"ignore\").decode()\n    return re.sub(r\"[^a-z0-9]+\", \"-\", text.lower()).strip(\"-\") or \"post\"\n\n\ndef init_posts(database: Path) -> None:\n    with connect(database) as conn:\n        conn.execute(_SCHEMA)\n\n\ndef get_post(database: Path, post_id: int) -> sqlite3.Row | None:\n    with connect(database) as conn:\n        return conn.execute(\n            \"select posts.*, users.display_name as author_name from posts\"\n            \" join users on users.id = posts.author_id where posts.id = ?\",\n            (post_id,)).fetchone()\n\n\ndef list_posts(database: Path) -> list[sqlite3.Row]:\n    with connect(database) as conn:\n        return conn.execute(\n            \"select posts.*, users.display_name as author_name from posts\"\n            \" join users on users.id = posts.author_id\"\n            \" order by posts.updated_at desc, posts.id desc\").fetchall()\n\n\ndef create_post(database: Path, *, author, title: str, slug: str, body: str,\n                publish: bool = False) -> int:\n    stamp = now()\n    try:\n        with connect(database) as conn:\n            cur = conn.execute(\n                \"insert into posts (title, slug, body, author_id, created_at,\"\n                \" updated_at) values (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?)\",\n                (title, slug or slugify(title), body, author[\"id\"], stamp, stamp))\n            post_id = cur.lastrowid\n            if publish:\n                _publish(conn, post_id, author, stamp)\n            return post_id\n    except sqlite3.IntegrityError as exc:\n        if \"posts.slug\" in str(exc):\n            raise DuplicateSlug from exc\n        raise\n\n\ndef update_post(database: Path, post_id: int, *, title: str, body: str,\n                slug: str | None, action: str) -> None:\n    \"\"\"Save changes. `slug` is None when the caller may not change it.\"\"\"\n    stamp = now()\n    try:\n        with connect(database) as conn:\n            if slug is None:\n                conn.execute(\"update posts set title=?, body=?, updated_at=?\"\n                             \" where id=?\", (title, body, stamp, post_id))\n            else:\n                conn.execute(\"update posts set title=?, body=?, slug=?,\"\n                             \" updated_at=? where id=?\",\n                             (title, body, slug or slugify(title), stamp, post_id))\n            if action == \"publish\":\n                author = conn.execute(\n                    \"select users.* from users join posts\"\n                    \" on posts.author_id = users.id where posts.id=?\",\n                    (post_id,)).fetchone()\n                _publish(conn, post_id, author, stamp)\n            elif action == \"draft\":\n                conn.execute(\"update posts set status='draft' where id=?\",\n                             (post_id,))\n    except sqlite3.IntegrityError as exc:\n        if \"posts.slug\" in str(exc):\n            raise DuplicateSlug from exc\n        raise\n\n\ndef _publish(conn: sqlite3.Connection, post_id: int, author, stamp: str) -> None:\n    first = conn.execute(\"select published_at from posts where id=?\",\n                         (post_id,)).fetchone()[\"published_at\"] is None\n    conn.execute(\"update posts set status='published' where id=?\", (post_id,))\n    if first:  # published timestamp, Byline and Slug lock are fixed at first publish\n        conn.execute(\n            \"update posts set published_at=?, slug_locked=1, byline_name=?,\"\n            \" byline_title=?, byline_term=? where id=?\",\n            (stamp, author[\"display_name\"], author[\"title\"], author[\"term\"],\n             post_id))\n\n\ndef unlock_slug(database: Path, post_id: int) -> None:\n    with connect(database) as conn:\n        conn.execute(\"update posts set slug_locked=0 where id=?\", (post_id,))\n\n\ndef delete_post(database: Path, post_id: int) -> None:\n    with connect(database) as conn:\n        conn.execute(\"delete from posts where id=?\", (post_id,))\n\n\ndef byline(post) -> str:\n    parts = [post[\"byline_name\"], post[\"byline_title\"], post[\"byline_term\"]]\n    return \", \".join(p for p in parts if p)\nEOF\ncat templates/base.html","description":"Write markdown, authz, posts
