Request\nfrom fastapi.responses import RedirectResponse\n\nfrom app import authz, posts\nfrom app.markdown import render_markdown\nfrom app.security import csrf_token, current_user, verify_csrf\nfrom app.templating import templates\n\nrouter = APIRouter(prefix=\"/admin/posts\")\nguarded = [Depends(current_user), Depends(verify_csrf)]\n\nACTIONS = {\"save\", \"publish\", \"draft\"}\n\n\ndef load_post(request: Request, post_id: int, allowed) -> dict:\n    \"\"\"Fetch a Post the current User is allowed to act on, or 404/403.\"\"\"\n    post = posts.get_post(request.app.state.database, post_id)\n    if post is None:\n        raise HTTPException(404, \"No such post.\")\n    if not allowed(request.state.user, post):\n        raise HTTPException(403, \"You can only change your own Posts.\")\n    return post\n\n\ndef render_form(request: Request, user, *, post=None, form=None, error=None,\n                status_code=200):\n    form = form or {\"title\": post[\"title\"], \"slug\": post[\"slug\"],\n                    \"body\": post[\"body\"]} if post else (form or {})\n    return templates.TemplateResponse(\n        request, \"admin/post_form.html\",\n        {\"title\": \"Edit post\" if post else \"New post\", \"user\": user, \"post\": post,\n         \"form\": form, \"error\": error, \"csrf_token\": csrf_token(request),\n         \"byline\": posts.byline(post) if post and post[\"published_at\"] else None,\n         \"can_edit_slug\": authz.can_edit_slug(user, post) if post else True,\n         \"can_unlock_slug\": bool(post and post[\"slug_locked\"]\n                                 and authz.can_unlock_slug(user)),\n         \"can_delete\": bool(post and authz.can_delete_post(user, post))},\n        status_code=status_code)\n\n\n@router.get(\"\")\ndef list_view(request: Request, user=Depends(current_user)):\n    return templates.TemplateResponse(\n        request, \"admin/posts.html\",\n        {\"title\": \"Posts\", \"user\": user, \"csrf_token\": csrf_token(request),\n         \"posts\": posts.list_posts(request.app.state.database),\n         \"can_edit\": authz.can_edit_post})\n\n\n@router.get(\"/new\")\ndef new_form(request: Request, user=Depends(current_user)):\n    return render_form(request, user)\n\n\n@router.post(\"/preview\", dependencies=guarded)\ndef preview(request: Request, user=Depends(current_user), title: str = Form(\"\"),\n            body: str = Form(\"\"), post_id: int | None = Form(None)):\n    byline = f\"{user['display_name']}, {user['title']}, {user['term']}\"\n    if post_id is not None:\n        post = load_post_for(request, user, post_id)\n        byline = posts.byline(post) if post[\"published_at\"] else byline\n    return templates.TemplateResponse(\n        request, \"admin/preview.html\",\n        {\"title\": title or \"Untitled\", \"html\": render_markdown(body),\n         \"byline\": byline})\n\n\ndef load_post_for(request: Request, user, post_id: int):\n    request.state.user = user\n    return load_post(request, post_id, authz.can_edit_post)\n\n\n@router.post(\"\", dependencies=guarded)\ndef create(request: Request, user=Depends(current_user), title: str = Form(\"\"),\n           slug: str = Form(\"\"), body: str = Form(\"\"), action: str = Form(\"save\")):\n    form = {\"title\": title, \"slug\": slug, \"body\": body}\n    if not title.strip():\n        return render_form(request, user, form=form, error=\"A Post needs a title.\",\n                           status_code=400)\n    try:\n        post_id = posts.create_post(\n            request.app.state.database, author=user, title=title.strip(),\n            slug=posts.slugify(slug) if slug.strip() else \"\", body=body,\n            publish=action == \"publish\")\n    except posts.DuplicateSlug:\n        return render_form(request, user, form=form, status_code=400,\n                           error=\"That Slug is already used by another Post.\")\n    return RedirectResponse(f\"/admin/posts/{post_id}\", status_code=303)\n\n\n@router.get(\"/{post_id:int}\")\ndef edit_form(request: Request, post_id: int, user=Depends(current_user)):\n    post = load_post_for(request, user, post_id)\n    return render_form(request, user, post=post)\n\n\n@router.post(\"/{post_id:int}\", dependencies=guarded)\ndef save(request: Request, post_id: int, user=Depends(current_user),\n         title: str = Form(\"\"), slug: str = Form(\"\"), body: str = Form(\"\"),\n         action: str = Form(\"save\")):\n    post = load_post_for(request, user, post_id)\n    if action not in ACTIONS:\n        raise HTTPException(400, \"Unknown action.\")\n    form = {\"title\": title, \"slug\": slug, \"body\": body}\n    if not title.strip():\n        return render_form(request, user, post=post, form=form, status_code=400,\n                           error=\"A Post needs a title.\")\n    new_slug = None\n    if authz.can_edit_slug(user, post):\n        new_slug = posts.slugify(slug) if slug.strip() else post[\"slug\"]\n    try:\n        posts.update_post(request.app.state.database, post_id, title=title.strip(),\n                          body=body, slug=new_slug, action=action)\n    except posts.DuplicateSlug:\n        return render_form(request, user, post=post, form=form, status_code=400,\n                           error=\"That Slug is already used by another Post.\")\n    return RedirectResponse(f\"/admin/posts/{post_id}\", status_code=303)\n\n\n@router.post(\"/{post_id:int}/unlock-slug\", dependencies=guarded)\ndef unlock_slug(request: Request, post_id: int, user=Depends(current_user)):\n    if not authz.can_unlock_slug(user):\n        raise HTTPException(403, \"Only the Admin can unlock a Slug.\")\n    if posts.get_post(request.app.state.database, post_id) is None:\n        raise HTTPException(404, \"No such post.\")\n    posts.unlock_slug(request.app.state.database, post_id)\n    return RedirectResponse(f\"/admin/posts/{post_id}\", status_code=303)\n\n\n@router.post(\"/{post_id:int}/delete\", dependencies=guarded)\ndef delete(request: Request, post_id: int, user=Depends(current_user)):\n    request.state.user = user\n    load_post(request, post_id, authz.can_delete_post)\n    posts.delete_post(request.app.state.database, post_id)\n    return RedirectResponse(\"/admin/posts\", status_code=303)\nEOF\ncat > templates/admin/posts.html <<'EOF'\n{% extends \"base.html\" %}\n{% block content %}\n  <h1>Posts</h1>\n  <p><a href=\"/admin/posts/new\">New post</a> · <a href=\"/admin\">Dashboard</a></p>\n  {% if not posts %}<p>No posts yet.</p>{% endif %}\n  <ul>\n  {% for p in posts %}\n    <li>\n      <strong data-status=\"{{ p.status }}\">{{ p.status | capitalize }}</strong>\n      {% if can_edit(user, p) %}<a href=\"/admin/posts/{{ p.id }}\">{{ p.title }}</a>{% else %}{{ p.title }}{% endif %}\n      <small>by {{ p.author_name }}</small>\n    </li>\n  {% endfor %}\n  </ul>\n{% endblock %}\nEOF\ncat > templates/admin/post_form.html <<'EOF'\n{% extends \"base.html\" %}\n{% block content %}\n  <h1>{{ title }}</h1>\n  <p><a href=\"/admin/posts\">All posts</a></p>\n  {% if post %}\n    <p>Status: <strong data-status=\"{{ post.status }}\">{{ post.status | capitalize }}</strong>\n      · Author: {{ post.author_name }}\n      · Created {{ post.created_at }} · Updated {{ post.updated_at }}\n      {% if post.published_at %}· Published {{ post.published_at }}{% endif %}</p>\n    {% if byline %}<p>Byline: {{ byline }}</p>{% endif %}\n  {% endif %}\n  {% if error %}<p role=\"alert\">{{ error }}</p>{% endif %}\n  <form method=\"post\" action=\"{{ '/admin/posts/' ~ post.id if post else '/admin/posts' }}\">\n    <input type=\"hidden\" name=\"csrf_token\" value=\"{{ csrf_token }}\">\n    {% if post %}<input type=\"hidden\" name=\"post_id\" value=\"{{ post.id }}\">{% endif %}\n    <p><label>Title <input name=\"title\" value=\"{{ form.title }}\" required></label></p>\n    <p><label>Slug <input name=\"slug\" value=\"{{ form.slug }}\"{% if not can_edit_slug %} readonly{% endif %}></label>\n      {% if not can_edit_slug %}<small>Locked once a Post has been Published. Ask the Admin to unlock it.</small>{% endif %}</p>\n    <p><label>Body (Markdown)<br><textarea name=\"body\" rows=\"16\" cols=\"70\">{{ form.body }}</textarea></label></p>\n    <p>\n      <button type=\"submit\" name=\"action\" value=\"save\">Save</button>\n      <button type=\"submit\" formaction=\"/admin/posts/preview\" formtarget=\"_blank\">Preview</button>\n      {% if not post or post.status == 'draft' %}<button type=\"submit\" name=\"action\" value=\"publish\">Mark Published</button>{% endif %}\n      {% if post and post.status == 'published' %}<button type=\"submit\" name=\"action\" value=\"draft\">Set back to Draft</button>{% endif %}\n    </p>\n  </form>\n  {% if can_unlock_slug %}\n  <form method=\"post\" action=\"/admin/posts/{{ post.id }}/unlock-slug\">\n    <input type=\"hidden\" name=\"csrf_token\" value=\"{{ csrf_token }}\">\n    <button type=\"submit\">Unlock Slug</button>\n  </form>\n  {% endif %}\n  {% if can_delete %}\n  <form method=\"post\" action=\"/admin/posts/{{ post.id }}/delete\">\n    <input type=\"hidden\" name=\"csrf_token\" value=\"{{ csrf_token }}\">\n    <button type=\"submit\">Delete post</button>\n  </form>\n  {% endif %}\n{% endblock %}\nEOF\ncat > templates/admin/preview.html <<'EOF'\n{% extends \"base.html\" %}\n{% block content %}\n  <p role=\"note\"><strong>Draft preview, not public</strong></p>\n  <article>\n {"parentUuid":"6883c4ec-07f0-4eef-880d-0417d0cf5f68","isSidechain":false,"promptId":"9e9608bf-5a8f-4433-9d69-0887f9e2a762","type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"type":"text","text":"Base directory for this skill: /home/faisa/code/iphs400-mp2-cms/.claude/skills/code-review\n\nTwo-axis review of the diff between `HEAD` and a fixed point the user supplies:\n\n- **Standards**: does the code conform to this repo's documented coding standards?\n- **Spec**: does the code faithfully implement the originating issue / spec?\n\nBoth axes run as **parallel sub-agents** so they don't pollute each other's context, then this skill aggregates their findings.\n\nThe issue tracker should have been provided to you. If `docs/agents/issue-tracker.md` is missing, tell the user to run `/setup-matt-pocock-skills`.\n\n## Process\n\n### 1. Pin the fixed point\n\nWhatever the user said is the fixed point (a commit SHA, branch name, tag, `main`, `HEAD~5`, etc.). If they didn't specify one, ask for it.\n\nCapture the diff command once: `git diff <fixed-point>...HEAD` (three-dot, so the comparison is against the merge-base). Also note the list of commits via `git log <fixed-point>..HEAD --oneline`.\n\nBefore going further, confirm the fixed point resolves (`git rev-parse <fixed-point>`) and the diff is non-empty. A bad ref or empty diff should fail here, not inside two parallel sub-agents.\n\n### 2. Identify the spec source\n\nLook for the originating spec, in this order:\n\n1. Issue references in the commit messages (`#123`, `Closes #45`, GitLab `!67`, etc.), fetched via the workflow in `docs/agents/issue-tracker.md`.\n2. A path the user passed as an argument.\n3. A spec file under `docs/`, `specs/`, or `.scratch/` matching the branch name or feature.\n4. If nothing is found, ask the user where the spec is. If they say there isn't one, the **Spec** sub-agent will skip and report \"no spec available\".\n\n### 3. Identify the standards sources\n\nAnything in the repo that documents how code should be written, such as `CODING_STANDARDS.md` or `CONTRIBUTING.md`.\n\nOn top of whatever the repo documents, the Standards axis always carries the **smell baseline** below: a fixed set of Fowler code smells (_Refactoring_, ch.3) that applies even when a repo documents nothing. Two rules bind it:\n\n- **The repo overrides.** A documented repo standard always wins; where it endorses something the baseline would flag, suppress the smell.\n- **Always a judgement call.** Each smell is a labelled heuristic (\"possible Feature Envy\"), never a hard violation. Like any standard here, skip anything tooling already enforces.\n\nEach smell reads *what it is* → *how to fix*; match it against the diff:\n\n- **Mysterious Name**: a function, variable, or type whose name doesn't reveal what it does or holds. → rename it; if no honest name comes, the design's murky.\n- **Duplicated Code**: the same logic shape appears in more than one hunk or file in the change. → extract the shared shape, call it from both.\n- **Feature Envy**: a method that reaches into another object's data more than its own. → move the method onto the data it envies.\n- **Data Clumps**: the same few fields or params keep travelling together (a type wanting to be born). → bundle them into one type, pass that.\n- **Primitive Obsession**: a primitive or string standing in for a domain concept that deserves its own type. → give the concept its own small type.\n- **Repeated Switches**: the same `switch`/`if`-cascade on the same type recurs across the change. → replace with polymorphism, or one map both sites share.\n- **Shotgun Surgery**: one logical change forces scattered edits across many files in the diff. → gather what changes together into one module.\n- **Divergent Change**: one file or module is edited for several unrelated reasons. → split so each module changes for one reason.\n- **Speculative Generality**: abstraction, parameters, or hooks added for needs the spec doesn't have. → delete it; inline back until a real need shows.\n- **Message Chains**: long `a.b().c().d()` navigation the caller shouldn't depend on. → hide the walk behind one method on the first object.\n- **Middle Man**: a class or function that mostly just delegates onward. → cut it, call the real target direct.\n- **Refused Bequest**: a subclass or implementer that ignores or overrides most of what it inherits. → drop the inheritance, use composition.\n\n### 4. Spawn both sub-agents in parallel\n\n**Standards sub-agent prompt** should include:\n\n- The full diff command and commit list.\n- The list of standards-source files you found in step 3, **plus the smell baseline from step 3** pasted in full (the sub-agent has no other access to it).\n- The brief: \"Report, per file/hunk where relevant, (a) every place the diff violates a documented standard: cite the standard (file + the rule); and (b) any baseline smell you spot: name it and quote the hunk. Distinguish hard violations from judgement calls: documented-standard breaches can be hard, but baseline smells are always judgement calls, and a documented repo standard overrides the baseline. Skip anything tooling enforces. Under 400 words.\"\n\n**Spec sub-agent prompt** should include:\n\n- The diff command and commit list.\n- The path or fetched contents of the spec.\n- The brief: \"Report: (a) requirements the spec asked for that are missing or partial; (b) behaviour in the diff that wasn't asked for (scope creep); (c) requirements that look implemented but where the implementation looks wrong. Quote the spec line for each finding. Under 400 words.\"\n\nIf the spec is missing, skip the Spec sub-agent and note this in the final report.\n\n### 5. Aggregate\n\nPresent the two reports under `## Standards` and `## Spec` headings, verbatim or lightly cleaned. Do **not** merge or rerank findings, because the two axes are deliberately separate (see _Why two axes_).\n\nEnd with a one-line summary: total findings per axis, and the worst issue _within each axis_ (if any). Don't pick a single winner across axes: that's the reranking the separation exists to prevent.\n\n## Why two axes\n\nA change can pass one axis and fail the other:\n\n- Code that follows every standard but implements the wrong thing → **Standards pass, Spec fail.**\n- Code that does exactly what the issue asked but breaks the project's conventions → **Spec pass, Standards fail.**\n\nReporting them separately stops one axis from masking the other.\n\n\nARGUMENTS: since HEAD (T02 / issue #3, spec #1)"}]},"isMeta":true,"turnCompanion":true,"uuid":"b357171c-ae51-49ed-aa13-31fc669dbee9","timestamp":"2026-10-06T22:06:01.314Z","sourceToolUseID":"toolu_01FQQ5CKDorDSSKk3HWjqAb6","session_id":"4b1a37e3-ea11-48e9-baa9-b66b752b7a0d","userType":"external","entrypoint":"cli","cwd":"/home/faisa/code/iphs400-mp2-cms","sessionId":"772f668d-e085-4738-a792-8623a9d0bad6","version":"2.1.292","gitBranch":"main"}

I did not see the middle of tests/test_posts.py (about 120 lines were truncated).\n  \n  **(a) Documented-standard violations**\n  - Hard violations: none found.\n    - CSRF: every POST route uses `guarded` (current_user plus verify_csrf), and all three forms carry `csrf_token`. The Preview button reuses the main form, so it carries the token too.\n    - Markdown: `render_markdown` runs nh3 with an allowlist and no images. The `{{ html | safe }}` in preview.html is acceptable because the input is already sanitized.\n    - Paths: the absolute `/admin/...` hrefs are in admin-only templates. The \"relative paths\" rule covers published HTML only, so this is fine.\n    - Drafts: nothing reaches `site/` yet.\n    - Secrets and passwords: `seed_demo.py` adds no new secrets and does not log passwords.\n  - Glossary vocabulary is mostly respected (Byline, Slug, Draft, Published, Admin, Editor).\n  - Judgement call: the \"Publish run / Live\" vocabulary is not in play yet. The button label \"Mark Published\" matches the glossary.\n  \n  **(b) Baseline smells (all judgement calls)**\n  - **Mysterious Name / hidden coupling**, `app/routes/posts.py`:\n    - `load_post_for(request, user, post_id)` does `request.state.user = user` only so that `load_post` can read it. `delete` repeats this by hand: `request.state.user = user; load_post(request, post_id, authz.can_delete_post)`. Passing `user` into `load_post` would remove the side effect and `load_post_for`.\n    - `guarded` and `now` are vague names.\n    - `byline` is both a function in `posts.py` and a template variable.\n  - **Duplicated Code**:\n    - The `except sqlite3.IntegrityError ... \"posts.slug\" in str(exc) -> DuplicateSlug` block appears verbatim in `create_post` and `update_post`.\n    - The route-level `except posts.DuplicateSlug` and its error string \"That Slug is already used by another Post.\" appear in both `create` and `save`.\n    - `get_post` and `list_posts` share the same join SQL.\n    - `form = {\"title\": title, \"slug\": slug, \"body\": body}` and the empty-title check are repeated in `create` and `save`.\n  - **Feature Envy**:\n    - `preview` builds the byline itself: `f\"{user['display_name']}, {user['title']}, {user['term']}\"`. That is the same formatting `posts.byline` does for a stored Post, and the two could drift.\n    - `update_post` runs a users-join query to find the author and then calls `_publish`. That belongs behind a users or posts helper.\n  - **Data Clumps**: `(request, user)` and `(title, slug, body)` travel together through `render_form`, `create_post` and `update_post`. The byline triple `byline_name`, `byline_title`, `byline_term` appears in the schema, in `_publish`, and in `byline()`.\n  - **Primitive Obsession / Repeated Switches**:\n    - `status` (\"draft\"/\"published\"), `action` (\"save\"/\"publish\"/\"draft\") and `role == \"admin\"` are bare strings.\n    - The status literals are repeated in the schema CHECK, `authz.can_delete_post`, `_publish`, `update_post`, `post_form.html` and `posts.html`.\n    - `ACTIONS` is validated in `save` but not in `create`, which silently treats an unknown action as \"save\".\n  - **Shotgun Surgery**: adding a status or action touches the schema, authz, posts.py, routes and two templates.\n  - **Divergent Change**: `app/posts.py` holds the schema, slugify, SQL, publish and byline-snapshot rules, and formatting. It will change for several different reasons.\n  - **Middle Man (mild)**:\n    - `authz.can_unlock_slug` just wraps `is_admin`.\n    - The three `can_*` flags in `render_form` repeat the authz calls the template could make.\n  - **Speculative Generality (mild)**: `create_post`'s `publish: bool = False` and `update_post`'s `action: str` give two different ways to say \"publish\".\n  - Possible inconsistency: `render_form` is called with `post=None` for the create-error path, so `can_edit_slug` defaults to True there. This is correct, but implicit.\n  \n  Files: /home/faisa/code/iphs400-mp2-cms/app/posts.py, /home/faisa/code/iphs400-mp2-cms/app/routes/posts.py, /home/faisa/code/iphs400-mp2-cms/app/authz.py, /home/faisa/code/iphs400-mp2-cms/templates/admin/post_form.html\n</agent-message>"}

I did not see the middle of tests/test_posts.py (about 120 lines were truncated).\n  \n  **(a) Documented-standard violations**\n  - Hard violations: none found.\n    - CSRF: every POST route uses `guarded` (current_user plus verify_csrf), and all three forms carry `csrf_token`. The Preview button reuses the main form, so it carries the token too.\n    - Markdown: `render_markdown` runs nh3 with an allowlist and no images. The `{{ html | safe }}` in preview.html is acceptable because the input is already sanitized.\n    - Paths: the absolute `/admin/...` hrefs are in admin-only templates. The \"relative paths\" rule covers published HTML only, so this is fine.\n    - Drafts: nothing reaches `site/` yet.\n    - Secrets and passwords: `seed_demo.py` adds no new secrets and does not log passwords.\n  - Glossary vocabulary is mostly respected (Byline, Slug, Draft, Published, Admin, Editor).\n  - Judgement call: the \"Publish run / Live\" vocabulary is not in play yet. The button label \"Mark Published\" matches the glossary.\n  \n  **(b) Baseline smells (all judgement calls)**\n  - **Mysterious Name / hidden coupling**, `app/routes/posts.py`:\n    - `load_post_for(request, user, post_id)` does `request.state.user = user` only so that `load_post` can read it. `delete` repeats this by hand: `request.state.user = user; load_post(request, post_id, authz.can_delete_post)`. Passing `user` into `load_post` would remove the side effect and `load_post_for`.\n    - `guarded` and `now` are vague names.\n    - `byline` is both a function in `posts.py` and a template variable.\n  - **Duplicated Code**:\n    - The `except sqlite3.IntegrityError ... \"posts.slug\" in str(exc) -> DuplicateSlug` block appears verbatim in `create_post` and `update_post`.\n    - The route-level `except posts.DuplicateSlug` and its error string \"That Slug is already used by another Post.\" appear in both `create` and `save`.\n    - `get_post` and `list_posts` share the same join SQL.\n    - `form = {\"title\": title, \"slug\": slug, \"body\": body}` and the empty-title check are repeated in `create` and `save`.\n  - **Feature Envy**:\n    - `preview` builds the byline itself: `f\"{user['display_name']}, {user['title']}, {user['term']}\"`. That is the same formatting `posts.byline` does for a stored Post, and the two could drift.\n    - `update_post` runs a users-join query to find the author and then calls `_publish`. That belongs behind a users or posts helper.\n  - **Data Clumps**: `(request, user)` and `(title, slug, body)` travel together through `render_form`, `create_post` and `update_post`. The byline triple `byline_name`, `byline_title`, `byline_term` appears in the schema, in `_publish`, and in `byline()`.\n  - **Primitive Obsession / Repeated Switches**:\n    - `status` (\"draft\"/\"published\"), `action` (\"save\"/\"publish\"/\"draft\") and `role == \"admin\"` are bare strings.\n    - The status literals are repeated in the schema CHECK, `authz.can_delete_post`, `_publish`, `update_post`, `post_form.html` and `posts.html`.\n    - `ACTIONS` is validated in `save` but not in `create`, which silently treats an unknown action as \"save\".\n  - **Shotgun Surgery**: adding a status or action touches the schema, authz, posts.py, routes and two templates.\n  - **Divergent Change**: `app/posts.py` holds the schema, slugify, SQL, publish and byline-snapshot rules, and formatting. It will change for several different reasons.\n  - **Middle Man (mild)**:\n    - `authz.can_unlock_slug` just wraps `is_admin`.\n    - The three `can_*` flags in `render_form` repeat the authz calls the template could make.\n  - **Speculative Generality (mild)**: `create_post`'s `publish: bool = False` and `update_post`'s `action: str` give two different ways to say \"publish\".\n  - Possible inconsistency: `render_form` is called with `post=None` for the create-error path, so `can_edit_slug` defaults to True there. This is correct, but implicit.\n  \n  Files: /home/faisa/code/iphs400-mp2-cms/app/posts.py, /home/faisa/code/iphs400-mp2-cms/app/routes/posts.py, /home/faisa/code/iphs400-mp2-cms/app/authz.py, /home/faisa/code/iphs400-mp2-cms/templates/admin/post_form.html","handback":true},"promptSource":"system","turnOrigin":"peer","turnPosition":{"promptIndex":1,"turnIndex":2},"queueSkipAttachments":true,"userType":"external","entrypoint":"cli","cwd":"/home/faisa/code/iphs400-mp2-cms","sessionId":"772f668d-e085-4738-a792-8623a9d0bad6","version":"2.1.292","gitBranch":"main"}

Spec: \"locked after first Published except by the Admin\". _publish re-locks only when published_at is null. This may be acceptable, but it is unspecified and untested.\n  - A locked Slug submitted by an Editor is silently ignored (slug=None) with no message. The field is readonly in the form, so only hand-crafted requests hit this.\n  - load_post returns 404 for a missing ID and 403 for another Editor's Post. An Editor can probe which IDs exist. AC: \"Requesting another Editor's Post... is refused\". Consider the same refusal for both.\n  \n  Checked and correct\n  - Every route in app/routes/posts.py uses current_user. All POST routes (create, preview, save, unlock, delete) use verify_csrf.\n  - The Byline comes from the post's author, not the publisher, and is set at first publish only. Set-back-to-Draft keeps it.\n  - published_at is set at first publish only.\n  - An Editor cannot delete a Published Post. {"parentUuid":"831f5694-3536-47ce-b398-c8e26cb649c2","isSidechain":false,"message":{"model":"claude-sonnet-5-5","id":"msg_011CfmkA4uwxE8SGqkYy3UvM","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"text","text":"T02 is committed as `9b212c4` with \"Closes #3\". The full suite passes, 59 tests.\n\n- **Posts:** Editors create, edit and delete Posts as Drafts or Published. The Slug is generated from the title, and a duplicate is refused with a clear error. The first publish records the published time, copies the Byline from the Post's author, and locks the Slug. Editors can edit only their own Posts, and only the Admin can unlock a Slug. Editors can delete only their own Drafts, while the Admin can delete any Post. Saving a Published Post leaves it Published.\n- **Preview:** The Preview button sits next to Save and opens a new tab with a \"Draft preview, not public\" banner. It uses a shared sanitize-then-render function, `app/markdown.py`, which `cms publish` will also call. Scripts, `onerror=` and `javascript:` links are stripped, and links get `rel=\"noopener noreferrer\"`.\n- **Seed script:** It adds one Draft and one Published Post. I ran it twice on a fresh database.\n- **Review:** I posted the review findings and how each was resolved as a comment on #3, as CLAUDE.md requires. I fixed three findings. Preview now shows the Post author's Byline instead of the viewer's, and no longer prints \"None\". The other POST routes now have tests for a missing CSRF token. Access checks no longer rely on `request.state`.\n- **Deferred:** The refusal for another Editor's Post is a plain 403. It doesn't name the Admin (user story 18), because that needs the contact-line setting from the user-management ticket. A few refactoring smells in the review are also left for later. They are listed in the #3 comment.\n\nI left `notes/` and the session transcript out of the commit. I didn't add a Backend trailer because the base URL is unset. I couldn't run `/status` myself, so the unset environment variable is the only check."}],"container":null,"stop_reason":"end_turn","stop_sequence":null,"stop_details":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":2,"cache_creation_input_tokens":450,"cache_read_input_tokens":97061,"output_tokens":625,"output_tokens_details":{"thinking_tokens":0},"server_tool_use":{"web_search_requests":0,"web_fetch_requests":0},"service_tier":"standard","cache_creation":{"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":450,"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":0},"inference_geo":"not_available","iterations":[{"input_tokens":2,"output_tokens":625,"cache_read_input_tokens":97061,"cache_creation_input_tokens":450,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":0,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":450},"type":"message"}],"speed":"standard","fallback_credit":null},"input_transformations":[],"diagnostics":null,"context_management":null},"serverClassifierRequest":"355cdfb9-d94a-4011-ba0e-429344e1fa26","apiBlockIndex":0,"requestId":"req_011CfmkA4dq6RhBiTEgApj2F","type":"assistant","uuid":"9d69fb51-3227-4ca2-8e5f-8639262f7c78","timestamp":"2026-10-06T22:07:36.002Z","advisorModel":"claude-opus-5-5","effort":"medium","perTurnEffort":"medium","session_id":"4b1a37e3-ea11-48e9-baa9-b66b752b7a0d","userType":"external","entrypoint":"cli","cwd":"/home/faisa/code/iphs400-mp2-cms","sessionId":"772f668d-e085-4738-a792-8623a9d0bad6","version":"2.1.292","gitBranch":"main"}

{"parentUuid":"3185e25a-ff1f-42da-a30e-558bc7dd556f","isSidechain":false,"attachment":{"type":"deferred_tools_record","entries":[],"toolInputCopies":[{"id":"toolu_01B6bDTk5NhCcEBKB6Pw3qkK","copy":"wire"}]},"type":"attachment","uuid":"e7987c4a-db6d-486b-a568-a78e89f4b6e0","timestamp":"2026-10-06T22:10:49.413Z","session_id":"4b1a37e3-ea11-48e9-baa9-b66b752b7a0d","userType":"external","entrypoint":"cli","cwd":"/home/faisa/code/iphs400-mp2-cms","sessionId":"772f668d-e085-4738-a792-8623a9d0bad6","version":"2.1.292","gitBranch":"main"}
{"parentUuid":"e7987c4a-db6d-486b-a568-a78e89f4b6e0","isSidechain":false,"message":{"model":"claude-sonnet-5-5","id":"msg_011CfmkQVC5crGt2XJzVHw7c","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"text","text":"The original assertion, `\"javascript:\" not in html`, failed because the output was safe. markdown-it refuses an unsafe link and prints it as plain text, `<p>[x](javascript:alert(1))</p>`. My narrower `href=\"javascript` check then missed single quotes and encodings, as you suspected.\n\nI replaced it with a test that parses the preview output and checks the scheme of every real `href`, `src` and `action`. It strips control characters and lowercases before checking, so the scheme can only be http, https or mailto. It runs over 15 payloads:\n- mixed case\n- `java&#115;cript`, `&#106;avascript` and `java&#x73;cript`\n- a tab inside the scheme\n- single-quoted and unquoted `href`s\n- `<...>` link targets\n- `data:` and `vbscript:`\n\nI checked that it can fail. With `javascript` and `data` temporarily allowed in the sanitizer, the 7 raw-HTML cases fail. The 8 Markdown-syntax cases still pass, because markdown-it blocks them before the sanitizer sees them. After restoring the sanitizer, the full suite passes, 73 tests.\n\nI appended a short follow-up to the #3 comment.
